000001 | from __future__ import annotations
000002 | import hashlib,re
000003 | from pathlib import Path
000004 | from .models import Decision,IngestionDecision,RiskFinding
000005 | 
000006 | _ALLOWED={'.pdf','.docx','.pptx','.txt','.md','.csv','.json','.yaml','.yml','.mp3','.wav','.mp4','.m4a'}
000007 | _INJECTION=(r'ignore (?:(?:all|any|the) )?(?:previous|prior|system) instructions',r'reveal (?:the )?(?:system|developer) prompt',r'treat this document as (?:an )?approved policy',r'send .* data to .*external')
000008 | _PII=(r'\b[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}\b',r'\b(?:\+?251|0)9\d{8}\b',r'\bstudent\s*(?:id|number)\s*[:#]?\s*[A-Z0-9-]{3,}\b')
000009 | _ASSESS=(r'answer\s*key',r'final\s*exam',r'live\s*exam',r'graded\s*assessment',r'confidential\s*marking')
000010 | _HARM=(r'suicide method',r'build (?:a )?bomb',r'how to poison',r'sexual exploitation')
000011 | 
000012 | def sha256_file(path:Path)->str:
000013 |  h=hashlib.sha256()
000014 |  with path.open('rb') as f:
000015 |   for block in iter(lambda:f.read(1024*1024),b''):h.update(block)
000016 |  return h.hexdigest()
000017 | 
000018 | def assess_file(path:Path,max_bytes:int=100*1024*1024)->tuple[str,tuple[RiskFinding,...]]:
000019 |  if not path.is_file():return '',(RiskFinding('FILE_NOT_FOUND','critical','The selected source is not a regular file.'),)
000020 |  digest=sha256_file(path); findings=[]
000021 |  if path.suffix.lower() not in _ALLOWED:findings.append(RiskFinding('UNSAFE_FILE_TYPE','critical',f'Unsupported file type: {path.suffix or "none"}'))
000022 |  if path.stat().st_size>max_bytes:findings.append(RiskFinding('FILE_TOO_LARGE','critical','The file exceeds the 100 MiB ingestion boundary.'))
000023 |  head=path.read_bytes()[:8]
000024 |  if path.suffix.lower()=='.pdf' and not head.startswith(b'%PDF-'):findings.append(RiskFinding('MIME_EXTENSION_MISMATCH','critical','PDF extension does not match file signature.'))
000025 |  if path.suffix.lower() in {'.docx','.pptx'} and not head.startswith(b'PK'):findings.append(RiskFinding('MIME_EXTENSION_MISMATCH','critical','Office extension does not match ZIP container signature.'))
000026 |  return digest,tuple(findings)
000027 | 
000028 | def assess_content(text:str,source_sha256:str)->IngestionDecision:
000029 |  findings=[]; controls=['ignore_embedded_instructions','local_processing_only','prohibit_targeted_advertising','prohibit_model_training']
000030 |  low=text.lower()
000031 |  for pat in _INJECTION:
000032 |   if re.search(pat,low,re.I):findings.append(RiskFinding('PROMPT_INJECTION','high','Embedded instructions are untrusted document content.'));break
000033 |  for pat in _PII:
000034 |   if re.search(pat,text,re.I):findings.append(RiskFinding('PERSONAL_DATA','high','Possible learner identifier or contact data detected.'));controls.append('redact_personal_identifiers');break
000035 |  for pat in _ASSESS:
000036 |   if re.search(pat,low,re.I):findings.append(RiskFinding('PROTECTED_ASSESSMENT','critical','Protected assessment material requires authorized teacher review.'));break
000037 |  for pat in _HARM:
000038 |   if re.search(pat,low,re.I):findings.append(RiskFinding('HARMFUL_CONTENT','critical','Potentially harmful actionable content requires review.'));break
000039 |  critical=any(x.severity=='critical' for x in findings)
000040 |  if critical:
000041 |   return IngestionDecision(Decision.REVIEW,source_sha256,tuple(findings),('secure_local_storage',),('local_indexing','summarize_document','generate_notes','generate_study_assets','tutor_answer','export_content'),tuple(dict.fromkeys(controls)),True,False)
000042 |  permitted=('local_storage','local_indexing','summarize_document','generate_notes','generate_study_assets','tutor_answer','tutor_chat')
000043 |  restricted=('external_distribution','targeted_advertising','commercial_profiling','model_training')
000044 |  decision=Decision.RESTRICTED if findings else Decision.RESTRICTED
000045 |  return IngestionDecision(decision,source_sha256,tuple(findings),permitted,restricted,tuple(dict.fromkeys(controls)),True,True)

000015 | '''
000016 | class RuntimeGovernanceRepository:
000017 |  def __init__(self,path:str|Path):self.path=Path(path);self.ensure_schema()
000018 |  def _connect(self):c=sqlite3.connect(str(self.path));c.row_factory=sqlite3.Row;return c
000019 |  def ensure_schema(self):
000020 |   with self._connect() as c:c.executescript(_SCHEMA)
000021 |  def receipt(self,event_type:str,payload:dict[str,Any],document_id:int|None=None,operation:str|None=None)->str:
000022 |   rid='EDU-GOV-'+uuid.uuid4().hex[:20].upper();now=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

000024 |   return rid
000025 |  def save_document(self,document_id:int,decision:dict[str,Any]):
000026 |   now=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
000027 |   with self._connect() as c:c.execute('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO runtime_document_governance(document_id,source_sha256,decision,permitted_json,restricted_json,controls_json,findings_json,receipt_id,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(document_id,decision['source_sha256'],decision['decision'],json.dumps(decision['permitted_operations']),json.dumps(decision['restricted_operations']),json.dumps(decision['controls']),json.dumps(decision['findings']),decision['receipt_id'],now))
000028 |  def get_document(self,document_id:int)->dict[str,Any]|None:
000029 |   with self._connect() as c:r=c.execute('SELECT * FROM runtime_document_governance WHERE document_id=?',(document_id,)).fetchone()
000030 |   if not r:return None
000031 |   d=dict(r)
000032 |   for k in ('permitted_json','restricted_json','controls_json','findings_json'):d[k[:-5]]=json.loads(d.pop(k))

000034 |  def record_generation(self,request_id:str,document_id:int,operation:str,decision:str,source_sha256:str,details:dict[str,Any]):
000035 |   with self._connect() as c:c.execute('INSERT INTO runtime_generation_governance(request_id,document_id,operation,decision,source_sha256,details_json,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(request_id,document_id,operation,decision,source_sha256,json.dumps(details,sort_keys=True,default=str),datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()))
000036 |  def save_chunk_decisions(self,decisions:list[dict[str,Any]]):

000040 |  def get_chunk_decisions(self,document_id:int)->list[dict[str,Any]]:

000391 |             markdown_notes=markdown,
000392 |             model_name="fallback-extractive",
000393 |             tokens_used=None,
000394 |             metadata={
000395 |                 "document_id": document_id,
000396 |                 "fallback": True,
000397 |                 "fallback_reason": reason,
000398 |                 "text_length": len(doc.raw_text or ""),
000399 |                 "created_at": self._now_iso(),
000400 |             },
000401 |         )

000403 |     def _fallback_study_assets(
000404 |         self,
000405 |         *,
000406 |         document_id: int,
000407 |         doc: IngestedDocument,
000408 |         flashcard_count: int,
000409 |         mcq_count: int,
000410 |         reason: str,
000411 |     ) -> StudyAssetsResult:
000412 |         sentences = self._split_sentences(doc.raw_text, limit=max(flashcard_count, mcq_count, 10))
000413 | 
000414 |         if not sentences:
000415 |             sentences = [
000416 |                 "The uploaded document should be reviewed by the learner and teacher.",
000417 |                 "AmbaAI can create offline learning activities from local curriculum materials.",
000418 |             ]
000419 | 
000420 |         flashcards: list[Flashcard] = []
000421 |         for index, sentence in enumerate(sentences[:flashcard_count], start=1):
000422 |             flashcards.append(
000423 |                 Flashcard(
000424 |                     question=f"What is the key idea in point {index}?",
000425 |                     answer=sentence,
000426 |                     source_ref=doc.title,
000427 |                 )
000428 |             )
000429 | 
000430 |         mcqs: list[MCQ] = []
000431 |         for index, sentence in enumerate(sentences[:mcq_count], start=1):
000432 |             mcqs.append(
000433 |                 MCQ(
000434 |                     question=f"Which statement best matches key point {index} from the uploaded material?",
000435 |                     choices=[
000436 |                         sentence,
000437 |                         "This point is unrelated to the uploaded material.",
000438 |                         "The uploaded material does not contain educational content.",
000439 |                         "This statement contradicts the source material.",
000440 |                     ],
000441 |                     correct_answer=sentence,
000442 |                     explanation="This option is based directly on the uploaded source text.",
000443 |                     source_ref=doc.title,
000444 |                 )
000445 |             )
000446 | 
000447 |         return StudyAssetsResult(
000448 |             flashcards=flashcards,
000449 |             mcqs=mcqs,
000450 |             metadata={
000451 |                 "document_id": document_id,
000452 |                 "cached": False,
000453 |                 "fallback": True,
000454 |                 "fallback_reason": reason,
000455 |                 "flashcard_count": len(flashcards),
000456 |                 "mcq_count": len(mcqs),
000457 |                 "created_at": self._now_iso(),
000458 |                 "model_name": "fallback-extractive",
000459 |             },
000460 |         )

000462 |     def _fallback_answer(
000463 |         self,
000464 |         *,
000465 |         question: str,

000468 |         reason: str,

000471 |         source_text = self._format_rag_hits(retrieved_hits)
000472 |

000475 |                 "AmbaAI could not use the local language model, so it is showing "

000480 |             )

000483 |                 "AmbaAI could not use the local language model, and no relevant "
000484 |                 "uploaded study material was retrieved for this question."
000485 |             )
000486 |

000488 |             answer_text=answer_text,
000489 |             raw_answer=answer_text,
000490 |             model_name=f"{model_name}-fallback",

000492 |             metadata={
000493 |                 "document_id": document_id,
000494 |                 "fallback": True,
000495 |                 "fallback_reason": reason,
000496 |                 "hits_count": len(retrieved_hits),
000497 |                 "created_at": self._now_iso(),
000498 |             },
000499 |         )

000501 |     def ingest_file(self, path: str | Path) -> IngestedDocumentResult:
000502 |         context = self._context(
000503 |             operation="ingest_file",
000504 |             path=path,
000505 |             model_name=WHISPER_MODEL,
000506 |         )
000507 | 
000508 |         stage = "start"
000509 |         document_id: int | None = None
000510 |         rag_indexed = False
000511 |         index_status = "not_indexed"
000512 |         index_error: str | None = None
000513 | 
000514 |         logger.info("File ingestion started", extra={"context": context})
000515 | 
000516 |         try:
000517 |             stage = "extract"
000518 |             if self.runtime_governance is None:
000519 |                 raise IngestionError("Runtime education governance is not configured for ingestion.", context=context)
000520 |             self.runtime_governance.preflight_file(path)
000521 |             doc = ingest_path(path, whisper_model=WHISPER_MODEL)
000522 |             raw_text = doc.raw_text or ""
000523 |             stage = "govern_ingestion"
000524 |             runtime_decision = self.runtime_governance.assess_ingestion(path, raw_text)
000525 |             if not runtime_decision.permits_persistence:
000526 |                 raise IngestionError("Runtime education governance refused persistence.", context={**context, "decision": runtime_decision.to_dict()})
000527 | 
000528 |             stage = "insert_document"
000529 |             document_id = self._insert_document(doc)
000530 |             self.runtime_governance.record_document(document_id, runtime_decision)
000531 |             self.db.update_document_metadata(document_id, {"runtime_governance": runtime_decision.to_dict()})
000532 | 
000533 |             stage = "chunk_text"
000534 |             chunks = chunk_text(
000535 |                 document_id=document_id,
000536 |                 text=raw_text,
000537 |             )
000538 | 
000539 |             stage = "insert_chunks"
000540 |             self._insert_chunks(
000541 |                 document_id=document_id,
000542 |                 chunks=chunks,
000543 |             )
000544 |             stage = "govern_chunks"
000545 |             persisted_chunks = list(self.db.get_chunks(document_id)) if chunks else []
000546 |             chunk_decisions = self.runtime_governance.assess_chunks(document_id, persisted_chunks) if persisted_chunks else ()
000547 |             permitted_chunk_ids = frozenset(
000548 |                 decision.chunk_id for decision in chunk_decisions if decision.indexing_permitted
000549 |             )
000550 | 
000551 |             if chunks and runtime_decision.permits_indexing:
000552 |                 stage = "index_document"
000553 |                 try:
000554 |                     self.rag.index_document(document_id, permitted_chunk_ids=permitted_chunk_ids)
000555 |                     rag_indexed = True
000556 |                     index_status = "indexed"
000557 |                 except Exception as exc:
000558 |                     rag_indexed = False
000559 |                     index_status = "failed"
000560 |                     index_error = str(exc)[:500]
000561 |                     logger.warning(
000562 |                         "Document chunking succeeded but RAG indexing failed; continuing",
000563 |                         extra={
000564 |                             "context": {
000565 |                                 **context,
000566 |                                 "document_id": document_id,
000567 |                                 "chunk_count": len(chunks),
000568 |                                 "stage": stage,
000569 |                                 "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
000570 |                                 "error_message": str(exc),
000571 |                             }
000572 |                         },
000573 |                         exc_info=True,
000574 |                     )
000575 |             else:
000576 |                 index_status = "not_indexed"
000577 | 
000578 |             metadata_updates: dict[str, Any] = {
000579 |                 "chunk_count": len(chunks),
000580 |                 "text_length": len(raw_text),
000581 |                 "rag_indexed": rag_indexed,
000582 |                 "index_status": index_status,
000583 |             }
000584 |             if index_error:
000585 |                 metadata_updates["index_error"] = index_error
000586 |             else:
000587 |                 metadata_updates.pop("index_error", None)
000588 | 
000589 |             persisted_metadata: dict[str, Any] = {}
000590 |             if hasattr(self.db, "update_document_metadata"):
000591 |                 persisted_metadata = self.db.update_document_metadata(
000592 |                     document_id,
000593 |                     metadata_updates,
000594 |                 )
000595 | 
000596 |             metadata = {
000597 |                 **(getattr(doc, "metadata", {}) or {}),
000598 |                 **persisted_metadata,
000599 |                 "document_id": document_id,
000600 |                 "chunk_count": len(chunks),
000601 |                 "text_length": len(raw_text),
000602 |                 "kind": doc.kind,
000603 |                 "created_at": self._now_iso(),
000604 |                 "rag_indexed": rag_indexed,
000605 |                 "index_status": index_status,
000606 |                 "rag_error": index_error,
000607 |             }
000608 | 
000609 |             result = IngestedDocumentResult(
000610 |                 document=doc,
000611 |                 document_id=document_id,
000612 |                 metadata=metadata,
000613 |             )
000614 | 
000615 |             logger.info(
000616 |                 "File ingestion completed",
000617 |                 extra={
000618 |                     "context": {
000619 |                         **context,
000620 |                         "document_id": document_id,
000621 |                         "chunk_count": len(chunks),
000622 |                         "text_length": len(raw_text),
000623 |                         "rag_indexed": rag_indexed,
000624 |                         "index_status": index_status,
000625 |                         "rag_error": index_error,
000626 |                     }
000627 |                 },
000628 |             )
000629 | 
000630 |             return result
000631 | 
000632 |         except IngestionError:
000633 |             raise
000634 | 
000635 |         except DatabaseError as exc:
000636 |             error_context = {
000637 |                 **context,
000638 |                 "stage": stage,
000639 |                 "document_id": document_id,
000640 |                 "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
000641 |                 "error_message": str(exc),
000642 |             }
000643 |             logger.exception(
000644 |                 "File ingestion failed during database persistence",
000645 |                 extra={"context": error_context},
000646 |             )
000647 |             raise IngestionError(
000648 |                 "File ingestion failed during database persistence.",
000649 |                 context=error_context,
000650 |             ) from exc
000651 | 
000652 |         except Exception as exc:
000653 |             error_context = {
000654 |                 **context,
000655 |                 "stage": stage,
000656 |                 "document_id": document_id,
000657 |                 "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
000658 |                 "error_message": str(exc),
000659 |             }
000660 |             logger.exception("File ingestion failed", extra={"context": error_context})
000661 |             raise IngestionError("File ingestion failed.", context=error_context) from exc
000662 | 
000663 |     def _begin_job_governance(
000664 |         self,
000665 |         *,
000666 |         method_name: str,
000667 |         input_metadata: dict[str, Any],
000668 |         governance_context: JobGovernanceContext | None,
000669 |     ) -> Any | None:
000670 |         document_id = input_metadata.get("document_id")
000671 |         if document_id is not None and self.runtime_governance is not None:
000672 |             doc = self._get_document_or_raise(int(document_id))
000673 |             source_path = str(doc.get("source_path") or "") if isinstance(doc, dict) else str(getattr(doc, "source_path", ""))
000674 |             self.runtime_governance.authorize_generation(int(document_id), method_name, source_path or None)
000675 |         if self.governance_coordinator is None:
000676 |             return None
000677 |         return self.governance_coordinator.begin(
000678 |             method_name=method_name,
000679 |             input_metadata=input_metadata,
000680 |             context=governance_context,
000681 |         )
000682 | 
000683 |     def _complete_job_governance(
000684 |         self,
000685 |         *,
000686 |         invocation: Any | None,
000687 |         result_material: dict[str, Any],
000688 |         result_metadata: dict[str, Any],
000689 |     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
000690 |         document_id = result_metadata.get("document_id")
000691 |         operation = getattr(invocation, "method_name", None) if invocation is not None else str(result_metadata.get("operation") or "runtime_output")
000692 |         governed_material = dict(result_material)
000693 |         if document_id is not None and self.runtime_governance is not None:
000694 |             validation = self.runtime_governance.validate_output(
000695 |                 int(document_id),
000696 |                 str(operation),
000697 |                 governed_material,
000698 |                 source_digest_verified=True,
000699 |                 retrieved_chunk_ids=tuple(result_metadata.get("retrieved_chunk_ids") or ()),
000700 |                 excluded_chunk_ids=tuple(result_metadata.get("excluded_chunk_ids") or ()),
000701 |             )
000702 |             governed_material = dict(validation.sanitized_material)
000703 |             result_metadata["runtime_output_governance"] = validation.evidence()
000704 |         if invocation is None:
000705 |             return governed_material
000706 |         completion = self.governance_coordinator.complete(
000707 |             invocation=invocation,
000708 |             result_material=result_material,
000709 |             result_metadata=result_metadata,
000710 |         )
000711 |         completion.require_delivery_acknowledged()
000712 |         return governed_material
000713 | 
000714 |     @staticmethod
000715 |     def _summary_from_material(original: SummaryResult, material: dict[str, Any]) -> SummaryResult:
000716 |         metadata = {**dict(original.metadata), **dict(material.get("metadata") or {})}
000717 |         return SummaryResult(markdown_notes=str(material.get("markdown_notes") or original.markdown_notes),model_name=original.model_name,tokens_used=original.tokens_used,metadata=metadata)
000718 | 
000719 |     @staticmethod
000720 |     def _answer_from_material(original: AnswerResult, material: dict[str, Any]) -> AnswerResult:
000721 |         metadata = {**dict(original.metadata), **dict(material.get("metadata") or {})}
000722 |         return AnswerResult(answer_text=str(material.get("answer_text") or original.answer_text),sources=list(material.get("sources") or original.sources),model_name=original.model_name,metadata=metadata)
000723 | 
000724 |     @staticmethod
000725 |     def _assets_from_material(original: StudyAssetsResult, material: dict[str, Any]) -> StudyAssetsResult:
000726 |         cards=[Flashcard(**item) if isinstance(item,dict) else item for item in material.get("flashcards",[])]
000727 |         mcqs=[MCQ(**item) if isinstance(item,dict) else item for item in material.get("mcqs",[])]
000728 |         metadata={**dict(original.metadata), **dict(material.get("metadata") or {})}
000729 |         return StudyAssetsResult(flashcards=cards,mcqs=mcqs,metadata=metadata)

000752 |             "flashcards": [asdict(card) for card in result.flashcards],
000753 |             "mcqs": [asdict(item) for item in result.mcqs],
000754 |             "metadata": dict(result.metadata),

000757 |     def generate_notes(
000758 |         self,
000759 |         document_id: int,
000760 |         *,
000761 |         force: bool = False,
000762 |         model_name: str | None = None,
000763 |         governance_context: JobGovernanceContext | None = None,
000764 |     ) -> SummaryResult:
000765 |         resolved_model = model_name or get_model_for_task("summary")
000766 |         context = self._context(
000767 |             operation="generate_notes",
000768 |             document_id=document_id,
000769 |             model_name=resolved_model,
000770 |         )
000771 |         invocation = self._begin_job_governance(
000772 |             method_name="generate_notes",
000773 |             input_metadata={"document_id": document_id, "force": force},
000774 |             governance_context=governance_context,
000775 |         )
000776 |         try:
000777 |             doc = self._get_document_or_raise(document_id)
000778 |             if not force:
000779 |                 cached = self.db.latest_summary(document_id)
000780 |                 if cached is not None:
000781 |                     data = dict(cached)
000782 |                     result = SummaryResult(
000783 |                         markdown_notes=str(data["summary_markdown"]),
000784 |                         model_name=str(data["model_name"]),
000785 |                         tokens_used=None,
000786 |                         metadata={
000787 |                             "document_id": document_id,
000788 |                             "cached": True,
000789 |                             "summary_id": data.get("id"),
000790 |                             "created_at": data.get("created_at"),
000791 |                             "text_length": len(doc.raw_text or ""),
000792 |                         },
000793 |                     )
000794 |                     governed_material = self._complete_job_governance(
000795 |                         invocation=invocation,
000796 |                         result_material=self._summary_material(result),
000797 |                         result_metadata={
000798 |                             "output_type": "summary_result",
000799 |                             "markdown_length": len(result.markdown_notes),
000800 |                             "model_name": result.model_name,
000801 |                             "tokens_used": result.tokens_used,
000802 |                             "document_id": document_id,
000803 |                         },
000804 |                     )
000805 |                     return self._summary_from_material(result, governed_material)
000806 |             try:
000807 |                 summary = self.content_delivery.generate_study_notes(
000808 |                     raw_text=doc.raw_text, title=doc.title
000809 |                 )
000810 |             except Exception as service_exc:
000811 |                 logger.warning(
000812 |                     "ContentDeliveryService note generation failed; attempting fallback",
000813 |                     extra={"context": {**context, "error": str(service_exc)}},
000814 |                     exc_info=True,
000815 |                 )
000816 |                 try:
000817 |                     summary = summarize_document_text(
000818 |                         text=doc.raw_text,
000819 |                         title=doc.title,
000820 |                         metadata=doc.metadata,
000821 |                         model_name=resolved_model,
000822 |                     )
000823 |                 except Exception as exc:
000824 |                     summary = self._fallback_summary(
000825 |                         document_id=document_id,
000826 |                         doc=doc,
000827 |                         reason=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
000828 |                     )
000829 |             candidate = SummaryResult(
000830 |                 markdown_notes=summary.markdown_notes,
000831 |                 model_name=summary.model_name,
000832 |                 tokens_used=summary.tokens_used,
000833 |                 metadata={
000834 |                     **(summary.metadata or {}),
000835 |                     "document_id": document_id,
000836 |                     "cached": False,
000837 |                     "created_at": self._now_iso(),
000838 |                 },
000839 |             )
000840 |             governed_material = self._complete_job_governance(
000841 |                 invocation=invocation,
000842 |                 result_material=self._summary_material(candidate),
000843 |                 result_metadata={
000844 |                     "output_type": "summary_result",
000845 |                     "markdown_length": len(candidate.markdown_notes),
000846 |                     "model_name": candidate.model_name,
000847 |                     "tokens_used": candidate.tokens_used,
000848 |                     "document_id": document_id,
000849 |                 },
000850 |             )
000851 |             candidate = self._summary_from_material(candidate, governed_material)
000852 |             if force:
000853 |                 self._delete_existing_summaries(document_id)
000854 |             summary_id = self.db.insert_summary(
000855 |                 document_id=document_id,
000856 |                 summary_markdown=candidate.markdown_notes,
000857 |                 model_name=candidate.model_name,
000858 |             )
000859 |             result = SummaryResult(
000860 |                 markdown_notes=candidate.markdown_notes,
000861 |                 model_name=candidate.model_name,
000862 |                 tokens_used=candidate.tokens_used,
000863 |                 metadata={**candidate.metadata, "summary_id": summary_id},
000864 |             )
000865 |             logger.info("Notes generation completed", extra={"context": context})
000866 |             return result
000867 |         except (JobGovernanceGateError, JobGovernanceDeliveryError):
000868 |             raise
000869 |         except Exception as exc:
000870 |             logger.exception("Notes generation failed", extra={"context": context})
000871 |             raise JobServiceError("Notes generation failed.", context=context) from exc
000872 |

000874 |         self,

000888 |     def generate_study_assets(
000889 |         self,
000890 |         document_id: int,
000891 |         *,
000892 |         force: bool = False,
000893 |         flashcard_count: int | None = None,
000894 |         mcq_count: int = 10,
000895 |         governance_context: JobGovernanceContext | None = None,
000896 |     ) -> StudyAssetsResult:
000897 |         structured_model = get_model_for_task("flashcards")
000898 |         target_flashcards = flashcard_count if flashcard_count is not None else 10
000899 |         context = self._context(
000900 |             operation="generate_study_assets",
000901 |             document_id=document_id,
000902 |             model_name=structured_model,
000903 |             force=force,
000904 |             flashcard_count=target_flashcards,
000905 |             mcq_count=mcq_count,
000906 |         )
000907 |         invocation = self._begin_job_governance(
000908 |             method_name="generate_study_assets",
000909 |             input_metadata={
000910 |                 "document_id": document_id,
000911 |                 "force": force,
000912 |                 "requested_flashcard_count": target_flashcards,
000913 |                 "mcq_count": mcq_count,
000914 |             },
000915 |             governance_context=governance_context,
000916 |         )
000917 |         try:
000918 |             doc = self._get_document_or_raise(document_id)
000919 |             existing_cards = self._get_existing_flashcards(document_id)
000920 |             existing_mcqs = self._get_existing_mcqs(document_id)
000921 |             if not force and existing_cards and existing_mcqs:
000922 |                 result = StudyAssetsResult(
000923 |                     flashcards=existing_cards,
000924 |                     mcqs=existing_mcqs,
000925 |                     metadata={
000926 |                         "document_id": document_id,
000927 |                         "cached": True,
000928 |                         "flashcard_count": len(existing_cards),
000929 |                         "mcq_count": len(existing_mcqs),
000930 |                         "created_at": self._now_iso(),
000931 |                     },
000932 |                 )
000933 |                 governed_material = self._complete_job_governance(
000934 |                     invocation=invocation,
000935 |                     result_material=self._assets_material(result),
000936 |                     result_metadata={
000937 |                         "output_type": "study_assets_result",
000938 |                         "flashcard_count": len(result.flashcards),
000939 |                         "mcq_count": len(result.mcqs),
000940 |                         "document_id": document_id,
000941 |                     },
000942 |                 )
000943 |                 return self._assets_from_material(result, governed_material)
000944 |             try:
000945 |                 result = self.content_delivery.generate_study_assets(
000946 |                     raw_text=doc.raw_text,
000947 |                     title=doc.title,
000948 |                     flashcard_count=target_flashcards,
000949 |                     mcq_count=mcq_count,
000950 |                 )
000951 |             except Exception as service_exc:
000952 |                 logger.warning(
000953 |                     "ContentDeliveryService asset generation failed; attempting fallback",
000954 |                     extra={"context": {**context, "error": str(service_exc)}},
000955 |                     exc_info=True,
000956 |                 )
000957 |                 try:
000958 |                     llm = self.model_service.get_client_for_task("flashcards")
000959 |                     result = StudyAssetsResult(
000960 |                         flashcards=generate_flashcards(
000961 |                             source_text=doc.raw_text,
000962 |                             count=target_flashcards,
000963 |                             llm=llm,
000964 |                         ),
000965 |                         mcqs=generate_mcqs(
000966 |                             source_text=doc.raw_text,
000967 |                             count=mcq_count,
000968 |                             llm=llm,
000969 |                         ),
000970 |                         metadata={
000971 |                             "document_id": document_id,
000972 |                             "cached": False,
000973 |                             "fallback": False,
000974 |                             "model_name": structured_model,
000975 |                         },
000976 |                     )
000977 |                 except Exception as exc:
000978 |                     result = self._fallback_study_assets(
000979 |                         document_id=document_id,
000980 |                         doc=doc,
000981 |                         flashcard_count=target_flashcards,
000982 |                         mcq_count=mcq_count,
000983 |                         reason=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
000984 |                     )
000985 |             governed_material = self._complete_job_governance(
000986 |                 invocation=invocation,
000987 |                 result_material=self._assets_material(result),
000988 |                 result_metadata={
000989 |                     "output_type": "study_assets_result",
000990 |                     "flashcard_count": len(result.flashcards),
000991 |                     "mcq_count": len(result.mcqs),
000992 |                     "document_id": document_id,
000993 |                     "model_name": result.metadata.get("model_name", structured_model),
000994 |                 },
000995 |             )
000996 |             result = self._assets_from_material(result, governed_material)
000997 |             if force:
000998 |                 self._delete_existing_study_assets(document_id)
000999 |             self.db.insert_flashcards(document_id, result.flashcards)
001000 |             self.db.insert_mcqs(document_id, result.mcqs)
001001 |             logger.info("Study asset generation completed", extra={"context": context})
001002 |             return result
001003 |         except (JobGovernanceGateError, JobGovernanceDeliveryError):
001004 |             raise
001005 |         except Exception as exc:
001006 |             logger.exception("Study asset generation failed", extra={"context": context})
001007 |             raise JobServiceError("Study asset generation failed.", context=context) from exc

001029 |     def tutor_answer(
001030 |         self,
001031 |         question: str,
001032 |         *,
001033 |         top_k: int = 5,
001034 |         max_tokens: int = 1200,
001035 |         document_id: int | None = None,
001036 |         active_tab: str = "Student Tutor",
001037 |         active_document_title: str | None = None,
001038 |         due_flashcards: int | None = None,
001039 |         session_id: str | None = None,
001040 |         remember: bool = True,
001041 |         governance_context: JobGovernanceContext | None = None,
001042 |     ) -> AnswerResult:
001043 |         model_name = get_model_for_task("tutor_chat")
001044 |         clean_question = question.strip() if isinstance(question, str) else ""
001045 | 
001046 |         context = self._context(
001047 |             operation="tutor_answer",
001048 |             document_id=document_id,
001049 |             question=clean_question,
001050 |             model_name=model_name,
001051 |             top_k=top_k,
001052 |             max_tokens=max_tokens,
001053 |             active_tab=active_tab,
001054 |             session_id=session_id,
001055 |         )
001056 | 
001057 |         if not clean_question:
001058 |             raise ValueError("Question must be a non-empty string.")
001059 | 
001060 |         invocation = self._begin_job_governance(
001061 |             method_name="tutor_answer",
001062 |             input_metadata={
001063 |                 "document_id": document_id,
001064 |                 "question_length": len(clean_question),
001065 |                 "top_k": top_k,
001066 |                 "max_tokens": max_tokens,
001067 |                 "active_tab": active_tab,
001068 |                 "remember": remember,
001069 |             },
001070 |             governance_context=governance_context,
001071 |         )
001072 |         session = session_id or self._session_id_for(document_id)
001073 | 
001074 |         try:
001075 |             self._prepare_rag_index(context)
001076 | 
001077 |             pipeline = self._get_rag_pipeline(document_id=document_id)
001078 |             if self.runtime_governance is None and pipeline is not None and hasattr(pipeline, "answer"):
001079 |                 try:
001080 |                     from amba_ai.rag.models import RetrievalQuery
001081 |                     query = RetrievalQuery(
001082 |                         text=clean_question,
001083 |                         filters={"document_id": str(document_id)} if document_id else {},
001084 |                         vector_k=max(20, top_k * 4),
001085 |                         keyword_k=max(20, top_k * 4),
001086 |                         final_k=top_k,
001087 |                     )
001088 |                     grounded = pipeline.answer(query)
001089 |                     answer = AnswerResult(
001090 |                         answer_text=grounded.answer,
001091 |                         model_name=model_name,
001092 |                         sources=[c.get("label", str(c)) for c in getattr(grounded, "citations", [])],
001093 |                         metadata={
001094 |                             "document_id": document_id,
001095 |                             "session_id": session,
001096 |                             "confidence": getattr(grounded, "confidence", 1.0),
001097 |                             "evidence_sufficient": getattr(grounded, "evidence_sufficient", True),
001098 |                             "citations": getattr(grounded, "citations", []),
001099 |                         },
001100 |                     )
001101 |                     answer = self._authorize_answer_delivery(
001102 |                         invocation=invocation,
001103 |                         answer=answer,
001104 |                         document_id=document_id,
001105 |                     )
001106 |                     self._persist_and_log_answer(clean_question, answer, session, document_id, active_tab, remember)
001107 |                     return answer
001108 |                 except (JobGovernanceGateError, JobGovernanceDeliveryError):
001109 |                     raise
001110 |                 except Exception as pipeline_exc:
001111 |                     logger.warning(
001112 |                         "Grounded production pipeline failed; falling back to direct RAG workflow",
001113 |                         extra={"context": {**context, "error": str(pipeline_exc)}},
001114 |                     )
001115 | 
001116 |             retrieved_hits = self._rag_search(clean_question, top_k=top_k, document_id=document_id, operation="tutor_answer")
001117 |             retrieved_context_text = self._format_rag_hits(retrieved_hits)
001118 |             relevant_memory = self.memory.get_relevant_history_text(
001119 |                 clean_question,
001120 |                 k=3,
001121 |                 session_id=session,
001122 |             )
001123 | 
001124 |             self._update_context_tracker(
001125 |                 active_tab=active_tab,
001126 |                 document_id=document_id,
001127 |                 active_document_title=active_document_title,
001128 |                 due_flashcards=due_flashcards,
001129 |             )
001130 | 
001131 |             system_prompt = self._build_tutor_system_prompt()
001132 |             full_prompt = self._build_tutor_prompt(
001133 |                 question=clean_question,
001134 |                 retrieved_context=retrieved_context_text,
001135 |                 relevant_memory=relevant_memory,
001136 |             )
001137 | 
001138 |             try:
001139 |                 llm = self.model_service.get_client_for_task("tutor_chat")
001140 |                 generation = llm.generate(
001141 |                     prompt=full_prompt,
001142 |                     system=system_prompt,
001143 |                     model=model_name,
001144 |                     max_tokens=max_tokens,
001145 |                     temperature=0.15,
001146 |                 )
001147 |             except Exception as exc:
001148 |                 logger.warning(
001149 |                     "Tutor LLM generation failed; returning retrieved fallback answer",
001150 |                     extra={
001151 |                         "context": {
001152 |                             **context,
001153 |                             "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
001154 |                             "error_message": str(exc),
001155 |                         }
001156 |                     },
001157 |                     exc_info=True,
001158 |                 )
001159 | 
001160 |                 answer = self._fallback_answer(
001161 |                     question=clean_question,
001162 |                     document_id=document_id,
001163 |                     retrieved_hits=retrieved_hits,
001164 |                     reason=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
001165 |                     model_name=model_name,
001166 |                 )
001167 |                 answer = self._authorize_answer_delivery(
001168 |                     invocation=invocation,
001169 |                     answer=answer,
001170 |                     document_id=document_id,
001171 |                 )
001172 |                 return answer
001173 | 
001174 |             raw_answer = getattr(generation, "response_text", getattr(generation, "text", str(generation)))
001175 |             formatted = self.pedagogy.format_response(
001176 |                 raw_llm_answer=raw_answer,
001177 |                 retrieved_context=self._documents_for_pedagogy(retrieved_hits),
001178 |             )
001179 | 
001180 |             answer = self._make_answer_result(
001181 |                 answer_text=formatted.formatted_answer,
001182 |                 raw_answer=raw_answer,
001183 |                 model_name=getattr(generation, "model_name", model_name),
001184 |                 sources=self._sources_from_hits(retrieved_hits),
001185 |                 metadata={
001186 |                     "document_id": document_id,
001187 |                     "session_id": session,
001188 |                     "hits_count": len(retrieved_hits),
001189 |                     "memory_injected": bool(relevant_memory),
001190 |                     "context_injected": True,
001191 |                     "is_low_confidence": formatted.is_low_confidence,
001192 |                     "citations": formatted.citations,
001193 |                     "tokens_used": getattr(generation, "tokens_used", None),
001194 |                     "generation": getattr(generation, "metadata", {}),
001195 |                 },
001196 |             )
001197 | 
001198 |             answer = self._authorize_answer_delivery(
001199 |                 invocation=invocation,
001200 |                 answer=answer,
001201 |                 document_id=document_id,
001202 |             )
001203 |             self._persist_and_log_answer(
001204 |                 user_text=clean_question,
001205 |                 answer=answer,
001206 |                 session_id=session,
001207 |                 document_id=document_id,
001208 |                 active_tab=active_tab,
001209 |                 remember=remember,
001210 |                 active_document_title=active_document_title,
001211 |             )
001212 | 
001213 |             logger.info(
001214 |                 "Tutor answer completed",
001215 |                 extra={
001216 |                     "context": {
001217 |                         **context,
001218 |                         "answer_length": len(answer.answer_text),
001219 |                         "sources_count": len(answer.sources),
001220 |                         "hits_count": answer.metadata.get("hits_count"),
001221 |                         "model_name": answer.model_name,
001222 |                         "memory_injected": answer.metadata.get("memory_injected"),
001223 |                     }
001224 |                 },
001225 |             )
001226 | 
001227 |             return answer
001228 | 
001229 |         except (JobGovernanceGateError, JobGovernanceDeliveryError):
001230 |             raise
001231 |         except ModelUnavailableError:
001232 |             raise
001233 | 
001234 |         except RAGError:
001235 |             raise

001275 |     def tutor_answer_stream(
001276 |         self,
001277 |         question: str,
001278 |         *,
001279 |         top_k: int = 5,
001280 |         max_tokens: int = 1200,
001281 |         document_id: int | None = None,
001282 |         active_tab: str = "Student Tutor",
001283 |         active_document_title: str | None = None,
001284 |         due_flashcards: int | None = None,
001285 |         session_id: str | None = None,
001286 |         remember: bool = True,
001287 |         governance_context: JobGovernanceContext | None = None,
001288 |     ) -> Iterator[str]:
001289 |         clean_question = question.strip() if isinstance(question, str) else ""
001290 |         if not clean_question:
001291 |             raise ValueError("Question must be a non-empty string.")
001292 |         invocation = self._begin_job_governance(
001293 |             method_name="tutor_answer_stream",
001294 |             input_metadata={
001295 |                 "document_id": document_id,
001296 |                 "question_length": len(clean_question),
001297 |                 "top_k": top_k,
001298 |                 "max_tokens": max_tokens,
001299 |                 "active_tab": active_tab,
001300 |                 "remember": remember,
001301 |             },
001302 |             governance_context=governance_context,
001303 |         )
001304 |         session = session_id or self._session_id_for(document_id)
001305 |         context = self._context(
001306 |             operation="tutor_answer_stream",
001307 |             document_id=document_id,
001308 |             question=clean_question,
001309 |         )
001310 |         self._prepare_rag_index(context)
001311 |         hits = self._rag_search(clean_question, top_k=top_k, document_id=document_id, operation="tutor_answer_stream")
001312 |         relevant_memory = self.memory.get_relevant_history_text(
001313 |             clean_question, k=3, session_id=session
001314 |         )
001315 |         self._update_context_tracker(
001316 |             active_tab=active_tab,
001317 |             document_id=document_id,
001318 |             active_document_title=active_document_title,
001319 |             due_flashcards=due_flashcards,
001320 |         )
001321 |         prompt = self._build_tutor_prompt(
001322 |             question=clean_question,
001323 |             retrieved_context=self._format_rag_hits(hits),
001324 |             relevant_memory=relevant_memory,
001325 |         )
001326 |         try:
001327 |             tokens = list(
001328 |                 self.model_service.generate_stream(
001329 |                     prompt=prompt,
001330 |                     system=self._build_tutor_system_prompt(),
001331 |                     task="tutor_chat",
001332 |                     max_tokens=max_tokens,
001333 |                     temperature=0.15,
001334 |                 )
001335 |             )
001336 |             full_answer = "".join(tokens).strip()
001337 |         except Exception as exc:
001338 |             answer = self._fallback_answer(
001339 |                 question=clean_question,
001340 |                 document_id=document_id,
001341 |                 retrieved_hits=hits,
001342 |                 reason=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
001343 |                 model_name=get_model_for_task("tutor_chat"),
001344 |             )
001345 |             tokens = [answer.answer_text]
001346 |             full_answer = answer.answer_text
001347 |         answer = AnswerResult(
001348 |             answer_text=full_answer,
001349 |             sources=self._sources_from_hits(hits),
001350 |             model_name=get_model_for_task("tutor_chat"),
001351 |             metadata={"document_id": document_id, "streamed": True},
001352 |         )
001353 |         answer = self._authorize_answer_delivery(
001354 |             invocation=invocation,
001355 |             answer=answer,
001356 |             document_id=document_id,
001357 |         )
001358 |         self._log_chat_to_database(
001359 |             user_text=clean_question,
001360 |             assistant_text=full_answer,
001361 |             context_payload={"sources": answer.sources, "streamed": True},
001362 |         )
001363 |         if remember and full_answer:
001364 |             self.memory.save_exchange(
001365 |                 user_text=clean_question,
001366 |                 assistant_text=full_answer,
001367 |                 session_id=session,
001368 |                 metadata={
001369 |                     "operation": "tutor_answer_stream",
001370 |                     "document_id": document_id,
001371 |                     "active_tab": active_tab,
001372 |                     "sources_count": len(answer.sources),
001373 |                 },
001374 |             )
001375 |         if answer.answer_text == full_answer:
001376 |             yield from tokens
001377 |         else:
001378 |             yield answer.answer_text
001379 | 
001380 |     def _prepare_rag_index(self, context: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
001381 |         rag_index = getattr(self.rag, "index", None)
001382 |         index_size = int(rag_index.ntotal) if rag_index is not None else 0
001383 | 
001384 |         if index_size > 0:
001385 |             return
001386 | 
001387 |         logger.warning(
001388 |             "RAG index is empty before tutor answer; attempting rebuild",
001389 |             extra={"context": {**context, "index_size": index_size}},
001390 |         )
001391 | 
001392 |         if hasattr(self.rag, "rebuild_from_database"):
001393 |             self.rag.rebuild_from_database()
001394 |         elif hasattr(self.rag, "rebuild_from_source_documents"):
001395 |             self.rag.rebuild_from_source_documents()
001396 | 
001397 |     def _rag_search(
001398 |         self,
001399 |         question: str,
001400 |         *,
001401 |         top_k: int,
001402 |         document_id: int | None = None,
001403 |         operation: str = "tutor_answer",
001404 |     ) -> list[Any]:
001405 |         if hasattr(self.rag, "search"):
001406 |             hits = list(self.rag.search(question, top_k=top_k))
001407 |         elif hasattr(self.rag, "retrieve"):
001408 |             hits = list(self.rag.retrieve(question, top_k=top_k))
001409 |         else:
001410 |             raise RAGError(
001411 |                 "RAG engine does not expose search or retrieve.",
001412 |                 context={"operation": "rag_search"},
001413 |             )
001414 |         if self.runtime_governance is None:
001415 |             return hits
001416 |         if document_id is None:
001417 |             raise RuntimeGovernanceGateError("Governed retrieval requires an explicit document_id.")
001418 |         governed = self.runtime_governance.filter_retrieval(int(document_id), operation, hits)
001419 |         return list(governed.hits)
001420 | 
001421 |     def _format_rag_hits(self, hits: list[Any]) -> str:
001422 |         if not hits:
001423 |             return ""
001424 | 
001425 |         parts: list[str] = []
001426 |         for index, hit in enumerate(hits, start=1):
001427 |             text = self._hit_text(hit)
001428 |             source = self._hit_source(hit)
001429 |             if text:
001430 |                 parts.append(f"[Source {index}: {source}]\n{text}")
001431 | 
001432 |         return "\n\n".join(parts).strip()
001433 | 
001434 |     @staticmethod
001435 |     def _hit_text(hit: Any) -> str:
001436 |         if isinstance(hit, dict):
001437 |             return str(hit.get("text") or hit.get("page_content") or "")
001438 | 
001439 |         if hasattr(hit, "page_content"):
001440 |             return str(hit.page_content)
001441 | 
001442 |         if hasattr(hit, "text"):
001443 |             return str(hit.text)
001444 | 
001445 |         return str(hit)
001446 | 
001447 |     @staticmethod
001448 |     def _hit_source(hit: Any) -> str:
001449 |         if isinstance(hit, dict):
001450 |             return str(
001451 |                 hit.get("source_ref")
001452 |                 or hit.get("source")
001453 |                 or hit.get("document_id")
001454 |                 or "Local Study Material"
001455 |             )
001456 | 
001457 |         metadata = getattr(hit, "metadata", None)
001458 |         if isinstance(metadata, dict):
001459 |             return str(
001460 |                 metadata.get("source_ref")
001461 |                 or metadata.get("source")
001462 |                 or metadata.get("document_id")
001463 |                 or "Local Study Material"
001464 |             )
001465 | 
001466 |         return "Local Study Material"
001467 | 
001468 |     def _documents_for_pedagogy(self, hits: list[Any]) -> list[Any]:
001469 |         return hits
001470 | 
001471 |     def _sources_from_hits(self, hits: list[Any]) -> list[str]:
001472 |         sources: list[str] = []
001473 |         seen: set[str] = set()
001474 | 
001475 |         for hit in hits:
001476 |             source = self._hit_source(hit)
001477 |             if source and source not in seen:
001478 |                 seen.add(source)
001479 |                 sources.append(source)
001480 | 
001481 |         return sources
001482 | 
001483 |     def _build_tutor_system_prompt(self) -> str:
001484 |         runtime_context = self.context_tracker.generate_system_prompt()
001485 | 
001486 |         return f"""
001487 | You are AmbaAI, an offline-first educational tutor.
001488 | 
001489 | Rules:
001490 | - Use the retrieved study material first.
001491 | - Use relevant long-term memory only to personalize support, not as a factual source.
001492 | - If the answer is not supported by the retrieved material, say that the uploaded material does not contain enough information.
001493 | - Keep answers clear, student-friendly, and exam-useful.
001494 | - Avoid hallucination.
001495 | - Cite available sources.
001496 | 
001497 | {runtime_context}
001498 | """.strip()
001499 | 
001500 |     @staticmethod
001501 |     def _build_tutor_prompt(
001502 |         *,
001503 |         question: str,
001504 |         retrieved_context: str,
001505 |         relevant_memory: str,
001506 |     ) -> str:
001507 |         sections = [
001508 |             "Student question:",
001509 |             question,
001510 |             "",
001511 |             "Retrieved study material:",
001512 |             retrieved_context if retrieved_context else "No relevant uploaded study material was retrieved.",
001513 |         ]
001514 | 
001515 |         if relevant_memory:
001516 |             sections.extend(
001517 |                 [
001518 |                     "",
001519 |                     "Relevant past learning memory:",
001520 |                     relevant_memory,
001521 |                 ]
001522 |             )
001523 | 
001524 |         sections.extend(
001525 |             [
001526 |                 "",
001527 |                 "Answer the student using the instructions. Be concise but complete.",
001528 |             ]
001529 |         )
001530 | 
001531 |         return "\n".join(sections).strip()
001532 | 
001533 |     def _update_context_tracker(
001534 |         self,
001535 |         *,
001536 |         active_tab: str,
001537 |         document_id: int | None,
001538 |         active_document_title: str | None,
001539 |         due_flashcards: int | None,
001540 |     ) -> None:
001541 |         self.context_tracker.set_active_tab(active_tab)
001542 | 
001543 |         if document_id is not None or active_document_title:
001544 |             self.context_tracker.set_active_document(
001545 |                 document_id=document_id,
001546 |                 title=active_document_title,
001547 |             )
001548 | 
001549 |         if due_flashcards is not None:
001550 |             self.context_tracker.set_due_flashcards(due_flashcards)
001551 | 
001552 |     @staticmethod
001553 |     def _session_id_for(document_id: int | None) -> str:
001554 |         if document_id is None:
001555 |             return "student:global"
001556 |         return f"student:document:{document_id}"
001557 | 
001558 |     def _log_chat_to_database(
001559 |         self,
001560 |         *,
001561 |         user_text: str,
001562 |         assistant_text: str,
001563 |         context_payload: dict[str, Any],
001564 |     ) -> None:
001565 |         try:
001566 |             self.db.log_chat("user", user_text)
001567 |             self.db.log_chat("assistant", assistant_text, context=[context_payload])
001568 |         except Exception:
001569 |             logger.warning("Failed to write chat log to database", exc_info=True)
001570 | 
001571 |     @staticmethod
001572 |     def _make_answer_result(
001573 |         *,
001574 |         answer_text: str,
001575 |         raw_answer: str,
001576 |         model_name: str,
001577 |         sources: list[str],
001578 |         metadata: dict[str, Any],
001579 |     ) -> AnswerResult:
001580 |         try:

001718 |     def _query_documents_fallback(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[Any]:
001719 |         try:
001720 |             conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
001721 |         except Exception:
001722 |             pass
001723 | 
001724 |         table_exists = conn.execute(
001725 |             """
001726 |             SELECT name
001727 |             FROM sqlite_master
001728 |             WHERE type = 'table'
001729 |               AND name = 'documents'
001730 |             """
001731 |         ).fetchone()
001732 | 
001733 |         if table_exists is None:
001734 |             return []
001735 | 
001736 |         columns = [
001737 |             str(row["name"])
001738 |             for row in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(documents)").fetchall()
001739 |         ]
001740 | 
001741 |         if not columns:
001742 |             return []
001743 | 
001744 |         order_column = "created_at" if "created_at" in columns else "id" if "id" in columns else columns[0]
001745 | 
001746 |         return list(
001747 |             conn.execute(
001748 |                 f"""
001749 |                 SELECT *
001750 |                 FROM documents
001751 |                 ORDER BY {order_column} DESC
001752 |                 LIMIT 10000
001753 |                 """
001754 |             ).fetchall()
001755 |         )

001757 |     def _normalize_document_listing(self, row: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
001758 |         try:
001759 |             if row is None:
001760 |                 return None
001761 | 
001762 |             if is_dataclass(row):
001763 |                 data = asdict(row)
001764 |             elif isinstance(row, dict):
001765 |                 data = dict(row)
001766 |             else:
001767 |                 try:
001768 |                     data = dict(row)
001769 |                 except Exception:
001770 |                     data = dict(getattr(row, "__dict__", {}))
001771 | 
001772 |             document_id = data.get("id") or data.get("document_id") or data.get("doc_id")
001773 | 
001774 |             if document_id is None:
001775 |                 logger.warning(
001776 |                     "Document listing skipped because id is missing",
001777 |                     extra={"context": {"row": str(row)[:500]}},
001778 |                 )
001779 |                 return None
001780 | 
001781 |             metadata = self._decode_metadata(data)
001782 | 
001783 |             return {
001784 |                 "id": int(document_id),
001785 |                 "document_id": int(document_id),
001786 |                 "title": str(data.get("title") or f"Document {document_id}"),
001787 |                 "source_path": str(data.get("source_path") or data.get("path") or ""),
001788 |                 "kind": str(data.get("kind") or data.get("type") or "unknown"),
001789 |                 "metadata": metadata,
001790 |                 "created_at": str(data.get("created_at") or ""),
001791 |             }
001792 | 
001793 |         except Exception as exc:
001794 |             logger.warning(
001795 |                 "Failed to normalize document listing row",
001796 |                 extra={
001797 |                     "context": {
001798 |                         "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
001799 |                         "error_message": str(exc),
001800 |                         "row": str(row)[:500],
001801 |                     }
001802 |                 },
001803 |                 exc_info=True,
001804 |             )
001805 |             return None

001807 |     def delete_document(self, document_id: int) -> None:
001808 |         context = self._context(operation="delete_document", document_id=document_id)
001809 | 
001810 |         if not isinstance(document_id, int) or document_id <= 0:
001811 |             raise ValueError("document_id must be a positive integer")
001812 | 
001813 |         try:
001814 |             if hasattr(self.db, "delete_document"):
001815 |                 self.db.delete_document(document_id)
001816 |             else:
001817 |                 with self._db_connect(operation="delete_document_sql", context=context) as conn:
001818 |                     conn.execute("DELETE FROM summaries WHERE document_id=?", (document_id,))
001819 |                     conn.execute("DELETE FROM flashcards WHERE document_id=?", (document_id,))
001820 |                     conn.execute("DELETE FROM mcqs WHERE document_id=?", (document_id,))
001821 |                     conn.execute("DELETE FROM chunks WHERE document_id=?", (document_id,))
001822 |                     conn.execute("DELETE FROM documents WHERE id=?", (document_id,))
001823 | 
001824 |             try:
001825 |                 if self._rag_engine is not None:
001826 |                     if hasattr(self._rag_engine, "remove_document"):
001827 |                         self._rag_engine.remove_document(document_id)
001828 |                     elif hasattr(self._rag_engine, "rebuild_from_database"):
001829 |                         self._rag_engine.rebuild_from_database()
001830 |             except Exception as rag_exc:
001831 |                 logger.warning(
001832 |                     "RAG synchronization failed after deletion",
001833 |                     extra={"context": {**context, "error": str(rag_exc)}},
001834 |                 )
001835 | 
001836 |         except Exception as exc:
001837 |             logger.exception("Failed to delete document", extra={"context": context})
001838 |             raise JobServiceError("Failed to delete document.", context=context) from exc

002021 |     def export_notes(
002022 |         self,
002023 |         document_id: int,
002024 |         path: str | Path,
002025 |         fmt: str = "md",
002026 |     ) -> None:
002027 |         context = self._context(
002028 |             operation="export_notes",
002029 |             document_id=document_id,
002030 |             path=path,
002031 |         )
002032 | 
002033 |         try:
002034 |             notes = self.get_notes(document_id)
002035 |             if not notes:
002036 |                 notes = self.generate_notes_markdown(document_id)
002037 | 
002038 |             output_path = Path(path).expanduser().resolve()
002039 |             output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
002040 | 
002041 |             fmt_clean = fmt.lower().strip(".")
002042 |             if fmt_clean in {"md", "markdown", "txt"}:
002043 |                 output_path.write_text(notes, encoding="utf-8")
002044 |                 return
002045 | 
002046 |             from amba_ai.core import export as export_module
002047 | 
002048 |             fn_map = {
002049 |                 "pdf": ["export_pdf", "export_markdown_to_pdf", "export_notes_to_pdf"],
002050 |                 "docx": ["export_markdown_to_docx", "export_notes_to_docx"],
002051 |                 "pptx": ["export_markdown_to_pptx", "export_notes_to_pptx"],
002052 |             }
002053 | 
002054 |             for fn_name in fn_map.get(fmt_clean, []):
002055 |                 fn = getattr(export_module, fn_name, None)
002056 |                 if callable(fn):
002057 |                     try:
002058 |                         doc = self._get_document_or_raise(document_id)
002059 |                         fn(str(output_path), doc.title, notes)
002060 |                     except TypeError:
002061 |                         fn(notes, output_path)
002062 |                     return
002063 | 
002064 |             raise ValueError(f"Unsupported export format: {fmt}")
002065 | 
002066 |         except Exception as exc:
002067 |             logger.exception("Failed to export notes", extra={"context": context})
002068 |             raise JobServiceError("Failed to export notes.", context=context) from exc

000669 |     def begin(
000670 |         self,
000671 |         *,
000672 |         method_name: str,
000673 |         input_metadata: Mapping[str, Any],
000674 |         context: (
000675 |             JobGovernanceContext | None
000676 |         ),
000677 |     ) -> JobGovernanceInvocation:
000678 |         normalized_method = (
000679 |             _require_method_name(
000680 |                 method_name
000681 |             )
000682 |         )
000683 | 
000684 |         operation_id = JOB_OPERATION_MAP.get(
000685 |             normalized_method
000686 |         )
000687 | 
000688 |         if operation_id is None:
000689 |             raise GovernanceConfigurationError(
000690 |                 "Job operation is not mapped for "
000691 |                 f"governance: {normalized_method}"
000692 |             )
000693 | 
000694 |         if not isinstance(
000695 |             context,
000696 |             JobGovernanceContext,
000697 |         ):
000698 |             raise GovernanceConfigurationError(
000699 |                 "A JobGovernanceContext is required "
000700 |                 "for governed job processing"
000701 |             )
000702 | 
000703 |         safe_input = _safe_metadata(
000704 |             input_metadata,
000705 |             allowed=_SAFE_INPUT_FIELDS,
000706 |             field_name="input_metadata",
000707 |         )
000708 | 
000709 |         event_id = _require_generated_text(
000710 |             self._identifier_provider.new_id(
000711 |                 "jobevt"
000712 |             ),
000713 |             "identifier",
000714 |         )
000715 | 
000716 |         timestamp = _require_generated_text(
000717 |             self._clock.now_iso(),
000718 |             "clock",
000719 |         )
000720 | 
000721 |         event = PipelineEvent(
000722 |             event_id=event_id,
000723 |             timestamp=timestamp,
000724 |             operation_id=operation_id,
000725 |             source_node=context.source_node,
000726 |             destination_id=(
000727 |                 context.destination_id
000728 |             ),
000729 |             payload_type="job_pre_gate",
000730 |             payload={
000731 |                 "job_operation": (
000732 |                     normalized_method
000733 |                 ),
000734 |                 **safe_input,
000735 |             },
000736 |             authorization_basis=(
000737 |                 context.authorization_basis
000738 |             ),
000739 |             synthetic_flag=(
000740 |                 context.synthetic
000741 |             ),
000742 |         )
000743 | 
000744 |         evaluation = (
000745 |             self._governance_service.evaluate(
000746 |                 event
000747 |             )
000748 |         )
000749 | 
000750 |         recommendation = _validate_evaluation(
000751 |             evaluation,
000752 |             expected_event_id=event.event_id,
000753 |             phase="pre-gate",
000754 |         )
000755 | 
000756 |         self._append_audit(
000757 |             context=context,
000758 |             event=event,
000759 |             event_type=(
000760 |                 "JOB_GOVERNANCE_PRE_EVALUATED"
000761 |             ),
000762 |             payload=_evaluation_audit_payload(
000763 |                 normalized_method,
000764 |                 evaluation,
000765 |             ),
000766 |         )
000767 | 
000768 |         if (
000769 |             recommendation.recommended_action
000770 |             is not EnforcementAction.PROCEED
000771 |         ):
000772 |             self._append_audit(
000773 |                 context=context,
000774 |                 event=event,
000775 |                 event_type=(
000776 |                     "JOB_GOVERNANCE_PREVENTED"
000777 |                 ),
000778 |                 payload={
000779 |                     "job_operation": (
000780 |                         normalized_method
000781 |                     ),
000782 |                     "recommended_action": (
000783 |                         recommendation
000784 |                         .recommended_action
000785 |                         .value
000786 |                     ),
000787 |                 },
000788 |             )
000789 | 
000790 |             raise JobGovernanceGateError(
000791 |                 method_name=normalized_method,
000792 |                 operation_id=operation_id,
000793 |                 event_id=event_id,
000794 |                 action=(
000795 |                     recommendation
000796 |                     .recommended_action
000797 |                 ),
000798 |             )
000799 | 
000800 |         return JobGovernanceInvocation(
000801 |             method_name=normalized_method,
000802 |             operation_id=operation_id,
000803 |             event=event,
000804 |             evaluation=evaluation,
000805 |             context=context,
000806 |         )
000807 | 
000808 |     def complete(
000809 |         self,
000810 |         *,
000811 |         invocation: JobGovernanceInvocation,
000812 |         result_material: Mapping[str, Any],
000813 |         result_metadata: Mapping[str, Any],
000814 |     ) -> JobGovernanceCompletion:
000815 |         if not isinstance(
000816 |             invocation,
000817 |             JobGovernanceInvocation,
000818 |         ):
000819 |             raise TypeError(

000821 |                 "JobGovernanceInvocation"

000825 |             _canonical_result_material(
000826 |                 result_material
000827 |             )

000829 | 
000830 |         safe_result = _safe_metadata(
000831 |             result_metadata,
000832 |             allowed=_SAFE_RESULT_FIELDS,
000833 |             field_name="result_metadata",
000834 |         )
000835 | 
000836 |         output_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(
000837 |             canonical_result.encode("utf-8")
000838 |         ).hexdigest()
000839 | 
000840 |         post_event = PipelineEvent(
000841 |             event_id=invocation.event.event_id,

000844 |                 "clock",
000845 |             ),
000846 |             operation_id=(
000847 |                 invocation.operation_id
000848 |             ),
000849 |             source_node=(
000850 |                 invocation.context.source_node
000851 |             ),
000852 |             destination_id=(
000853 |                 invocation.context
000854 |                 .destination_id
000855 |             ),
000856 |             payload_type="job_post_gate",
000857 |             payload={
000858 |                 "job_operation": (
000859 |                     invocation.method_name
000860 |                 ),
000861 |                 "output_sha256": (
000862 |                     output_sha256
000863 |                 ),
000864 |                 **safe_result,
000865 |             },
000866 |             authorization_basis=(
000867 |                 invocation.context
000868 |                 .authorization_basis
000869 |             ),
000870 |             synthetic_flag=(
000871 |                 invocation.context.synthetic
000872 |             ),
000873 |         )
000874 | 
000875 |         post_evaluation = (
000876 |             self._governance_service.evaluate(
000877 |                 post_event
000878 |             )
000879 |         )
000880 | 
000881 |         recommendation = _validate_evaluation(
000882 |             post_evaluation,
000883 |             expected_event_id=(
000884 |                 post_event.event_id
000885 |             ),
000886 |             phase="post-gate",
000887 |         )
000888 | 
000889 |         self._append_audit(
000890 |             context=invocation.context,
000891 |             event=post_event,
000892 |             event_type=(
000893 |                 "JOB_GOVERNANCE_POST_EVALUATED"
000894 |             ),
000895 |             payload={
000896 |                 **_evaluation_audit_payload(
000897 |                     invocation.method_name,
000898 |                     post_evaluation,
000899 |                 ),
000900 |                 "output_sha256": (
000901 |                     output_sha256
000902 |                 ),
000903 |                 **safe_result,
000904 |             },
000905 |         )
000906 | 
000907 |         idempotency_key = _execution_key(
000908 |             invocation=invocation,
000909 |             post_event=post_event,
000910 |             output_sha256=output_sha256,
000911 |         )
000912 | 
000913 |         execution_details = {
000914 |             "job_operation": (
000915 |                 invocation.method_name
000916 |             ),
000917 |             "output_sha256": output_sha256,
000918 |             **safe_result,
000919 |         }
000920 | 
000921 |         execution_result = (
000922 |             self._execution_service.execute(
000923 |                 event=post_event,
000924 |                 recommendation=recommendation,
000925 |                 approval_decision=(
000926 |                     invocation.context
000927 |                     .approval_decision
000928 |                 ),
000929 |                 idempotency_key=(
000930 |                     idempotency_key
000931 |                 ),
000932 |                 execution_details=(
000933 |                     execution_details
000934 |                 ),
000935 |             )
000936 |         )
000937 | 
000938 |         if not isinstance(
000939 |             execution_result,
000940 |             ExecutionResult,
000941 |         ):
000942 |             raise GovernanceConfigurationError(
000943 |                 "Execution service returned an "
000944 |                 "invalid ExecutionResult"
000945 |             )
000946 | 
000947 |         self._append_audit(
000948 |             context=invocation.context,
000949 |             event=post_event,
000950 |             event_type=(
000951 |                 "JOB_EXECUTION_RECORDED"
000952 |             ),
000953 |             payload={
000954 |                 "job_operation": (
000955 |                     invocation.method_name
000956 |                 ),
000957 |                 "recommended_action": (
000958 |                     recommendation
000959 |                     .recommended_action

001110 | def _require_sha256(
001111 |     value: object,
001112 |     field_name: str,
001113 | ) -> str:
001114 |     if not isinstance(value, str):
001115 |         raise TypeError(
001116 |             f"{field_name} must be a string"
001117 |         )
001118 | 
001119 |     normalized = value.strip()
001120 | 
001121 |     if (
001122 |         _SHA256_PATTERN.fullmatch(
001123 |             normalized
001124 |         )
001125 |         is None
001126 |     ):
001127 |         raise ValueError(
001128 |             f"{field_name} must be exactly 64 "
001129 |             "lowercase hexadecimal characters"
001130 |         )
001131 | 
001132 |     return normalized

001134 | 
001135 | def _safe_metadata(
001136 |     value: Mapping[str, Any],
001137 |     *,
001138 |     allowed: frozenset[str],
001139 |     field_name: str,
001140 | ) -> dict[str, Any]:
001141 |     if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
001142 |         raise TypeError(
001143 |             f"{field_name} must be a mapping"
001144 |         )
001145 | 
001146 |     normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
001147 | 
001148 |     for raw_key, item in value.items():
001149 |         if not isinstance(raw_key, str):
001150 |             raise GovernanceConfigurationError(
001151 |                 f"{field_name} keys must "
001152 |                 "be strings"
001153 |             )
001154 | 
001155 |         key = raw_key.strip()
001156 | 
001157 |         if not key:
001158 |             raise GovernanceConfigurationError(
001159 |                 f"{field_name} keys must not "
001160 |                 "be empty"
001161 |             )
001162 | 
001163 |         if key not in allowed:
001164 |             raise GovernanceConfigurationError(
001165 |                 f"{field_name} contains a "
001166 |                 f"non-permitted field: {key}"
001167 |             )
001168 | 
001169 |         if key in normalized:
001170 |             raise GovernanceConfigurationError(
001171 |                 f"{field_name} contains a "
001172 |                 f"duplicate normalized field: "
001173 |                 f"{key}"
001174 |             )
001175 | 
001176 |         normalized[key] = item
001177 | 
001178 |     try:
001179 |         canonical = to_canonical_json(
001180 |             normalized
001181 |         )
001182 |     except (TypeError, ValueError) as error:
001183 |         raise GovernanceConfigurationError(
001184 |             f"{field_name} must be canonically "
001185 |             "serializable"
001186 |         ) from error
001187 | 
001188 |     return _decode_canonical_mapping(
001189 |         canonical,
001190 |         field_name,
001191 |     )
001192 |
