000025 | 
000026 | class ModelService:
000027 |     """
000028 |     Central edge SLM authority for AmbaAI.
000029 | 
000030 |     Features:
000031 |     - Thread-safe singleton with lazy client pools.
000032 |     - Automatic task-to-model routing (Tutor, Summaries, Flashcards, MCQs).
000033 |     - Edge SLM health probing with fast timeout checks.
000034 |     - Low-latency token streaming support for student UIs.
000035 |     - Memory/VRAM conservation via model unloading.
000036 |     """
000037 | 
000038 |     _instance: Optional["ModelService"] = None
000039 |     _instance_lock = threading.Lock()
000040 | 
000041 |     def __init__(
000042 |         self,
000043 |         *,
000044 |         default_model: str = OLLAMA_TUTOR_MODEL,
000045 |         temperature: float = 0.2,
000046 |         context_length: int = 4096,
000047 |     ) -> None:
000048 |         self._lock = threading.RLock()
000049 |         self._clients: dict[tuple[str, float, int], OllamaClient] = {}
000050 |         self._active_model_name = str(default_model)
000051 |         self._temperature = float(temperature)
000052 |         self._context_length = int(context_length)
000053 |         self._last_health_check_time: float = 0.0
000054 |         self._is_healthy_cached: bool = False
000055 | 
000056 |         logger.info(
000057 |             "ModelService initialized",

000059 |                 "context": {
000060 |                     "default_model": self._active_model_name,
000061 |                     "temperature": self._temperature,

000063 |                 }
000064 |             },

000071 |                 cls._instance = cls()

000080 |     def get_llm(
000081 |         self,
000082 |         model_name: str | None = None,
000083 |         *,
000084 |         temperature: float | None = None,
000085 |         context_length: int | None = None,
000086 |     ) -> OllamaClient:
000087 |         """
000088 |         Return an active, thread-safe OllamaClient from the pool.
000089 |         """
000090 |         target_model = str(model_name or self._active_model_name)
000091 |         target_temp = float(self._temperature if temperature is None else temperature)
000092 |         target_ctx = int(self._context_length if context_length is None else context_length)
000093 | 
000094 |         cache_key = (target_model, target_temp, target_ctx)
000095 | 
000096 |         with self._lock:
000097 |             client = self._clients.get(cache_key)
000098 |             if client is None:
000099 |                 client = self._create_client(
000100 |                     model_name=target_model,
000101 |                     temperature=target_temp,
000102 |                     context_length=target_ctx,
000103 |                 )
000104 |                 self._clients[cache_key] = client
000105 |             return client

000198 |                 system=system,
000199 |                 task=task,

000201 |                 max_tokens=max_tokens,
000202 |                 temperature=temperature,
000203 |             )

000205 |             for i, word in enumerate(words):
000206 |                 yield word + (" " if i < len(words) - 1 else "")

000245 |     def is_healthy(self, force_check: bool = False) -> bool:
000246 |         """
000247 |         Check if the local Ollama daemon is reachable.
000248 |         Caches result for 10 seconds to avoid unnecessary socket polling.
000249 |         """
000250 |         now = time.time()
000251 |         if not force_check and (now - self._last_health_check_time < 10.0):
000252 |             return self._is_healthy_cached
000253 | 
000254 |         with self._lock:
000255 |             try:
000256 |                 client = self.get_llm()
000257 |                 if hasattr(client, "health_check"):
000258 |                     healthy = bool(client.health_check())
000259 |                 elif hasattr(client, "list_local_models"):
000260 |                     client.list_local_models()
000261 |                     healthy = True
000262 |                 else:
000263 |                     healthy = True
000264 |             except Exception as exc:
000265 |                 logger.warning("Local edge SLM health check failed: %s", exc)
000266 |                 healthy = False
000267 | 
000268 |             self._is_healthy_cached = healthy
000269 |             self._last_health_check_time = now
000270 |             return healthy
000271 | 
000272 |     def unload_model(self, model_name: str | None = None) -> bool:
000273 |         """
000274 |         Unload an edge model from VRAM/RAM (keep_alive=0).
000275 |         """
000276 |         target = model_name or self._active_model_name
000277 |         with self._lock:
000278 |             try:
000279 |                 client = self.get_llm(target)
000280 |                 if hasattr(client, "unload"):
000281 |                     client.unload()

000283 |                 return True
000284 |             except Exception as exc:
000285 |                 logger.warning("Failed to unload model %s: %s", target, exc)
000286 |                 return False
000287 | 
000288 |     def shutdown(self) -> None:
000289 |         """
000290 |         Release all pooled LLM client resources and clear caches.
000291 |         """
000292 |         with self._lock:
000293 |             logger.info("Shutting down ModelService client pool")
000294 |             for (_, _, _), client in self._clients.items():
000295 |                 if hasattr(client, "close"):
000296 |                     try:
000297 |                         client.close()
000298 |                     except Exception:
000299 |                         pass
000300 |             self._clients.clear()

000098 | class DefaultFallbackGenerator(FallbackGenerator):
000099 |     def extractive_notes(self, text: str, title: str) -> SummaryResult:
000100 |         paragraphs = [p.strip() for p in text.split("\n\n") if len(p.strip()) > 30]
000101 |         summary_body = (
000102 |             paragraphs[0]
000103 |             if paragraphs
000104 |             else "Source text successfully ingested and indexed for curriculum review."
000105 |         )
000106 |         body_notes = "\n\n".join(paragraphs[1:6]) if len(paragraphs) > 1 else summary_body
000107 | 
000108 |         markdown = (
000109 |             f"<!-- {SCHEMA_VERSION} -->\n"
000110 |             f"# {title}\n\n"
000111 |             f"## Executive Summary\n{summary_body}\n\n"
000112 |             f"## Structured Study Notes\n{body_notes}\n\n"
000113 |             f"## Sources\n- {title}"
000114 |         )
000115 |         return SummaryResult(
000116 |             markdown_notes=markdown,
000117 |             model_name="fallback-extractive",
000118 |             tokens_used=0,
000119 |         )

000121 |     def synthesize_flashcards(self, text: str, title: str, needed: int) -> list[Flashcard]:
000122 |         sentences = [
000123 |             s.strip()
000124 |             for s in re.split(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+", text)
000125 |             if 30 <= len(s.strip()) <= 200
000126 |         ]
000127 |         cards: list[Flashcard] = []
000128 | 
000129 |         for idx, sentence in enumerate(sentences[:needed], 1):
000130 |             cards.append(
000131 |                 Flashcard(
000132 |                     question=f"What key fact is established regarding section {idx} of {title}?",
000133 |                     answer=sentence,
000134 |                     source_ref=title,
000135 |                 )
000136 |             )
000137 | 
000138 |         while len(cards) < needed:
000139 |             idx = len(cards) + 1
000140 |             cards.append(
000141 |                 Flashcard(
000142 |                     question=f"What is the designated core objective for learning phase {idx}?",
000143 |                     answer=f"Verified study material establishes key mechanisms documented under {title}.",
000144 |                     source_ref=title,
000145 |                 )
000146 |             )
000147 | 
000148 |         return cards

000150 |     def synthesize_mcqs(self, text: str, title: str, needed: int) -> list[MCQ]:
000151 |         sentences = [
000152 |             s.strip()
000153 |             for s in re.split(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+", text)
000154 |             if 35 <= len(s.strip()) <= 180
000155 |         ]
000156 |         mcqs: list[MCQ] = []
000157 | 
000158 |         if len(sentences) < 4:
000159 |             sentences.extend(
000160 |                 [
000161 |                     f"The foundational concept is established in the primary documentation of {title}.",
000162 |                     f"Core analytical principles require systematic application of {title}.",
000163 |                     "Systematic verification relies on primary evidence established in the text.",
000164 |                     "The designated framework establishes validated conceptual boundaries.",
000165 |                 ]
000166 |             )
000167 | 
000168 |         for idx in range(needed):
000169 |             anchor_idx = idx % len(sentences)
000170 |             correct_choice = sentences[anchor_idx]
000171 | 
000172 |             pool = [s for j, s in enumerate(sentences) if j != anchor_idx]
000173 |             distractors = pool[:3]
000174 |             while len(distractors) < 3:
000175 |                 distractors.append(
000176 |                     f"This proposition contradicts the findings documented in {title}."
000177 |                 )
000178 | 
000179 |             choices = [correct_choice, distractors[0], distractors[1], distractors[2]]
000180 | 
000181 |             mcqs.append(
000182 |                 MCQ(
000183 |                     question=f"Which proposition represents the verified finding for section {idx + 1}?",
000184 |                     choices=[choices[0], choices[1], choices[2], choices[3]],
000185 |                     correct_answer=correct_choice,
000186 |                     explanation=f"The primary source text directly validates: {correct_choice}",
000187 |                     source_ref=title,
000188 |                 )
000189 |             )
000190 | 
000191 |         return mcqs

000229 |             else:
000230 |                 self.summary_failure_count += 1

000255 |                 + self.summary_failure_count

000263 |                 "asset_failure": self.asset_failure_count,
000264 |                 "fallback_count": self.fallback_invocations,
000265 |                 "circuit_trips": self.circuit_breaker_trips,
000266 |                 "total_tokens": self.total_tokens_consumed,
000267 |                 "total_requests": total_requests,
000268 |                 "avg_latency": round(
000269 |                     self.total_latency_seconds / max(1, total_requests),
000270 |                     3,
000271 |                 ),

000274 | 
000275 | class CircuitBreaker:
000276 |     def __init__(
000277 |         self,
000278 |         failure_threshold: int = 3,
000279 |         reset_timeout: float = 60.0,
000280 |         on_trip: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
000281 |     ) -> None:
000282 |         self.failure_threshold = failure_threshold
000283 |         self.reset_timeout = reset_timeout
000284 |         self.on_trip = on_trip
000285 |         self.failure_count = 0
000286 |         self.last_failure_time = 0.0
000287 |         self.state: str = "CLOSED"
000288 |         self._lock = threading.Lock()
000289 | 
000290 |     def record_success(self) -> None:
000291 |         with self._lock:
000292 |             self.failure_count = 0
000293 |             self.state = "CLOSED"
000294 | 
000295 |     def record_failure(self) -> None:
000296 |         tripped = False
000297 |         with self._lock:
000298 |             self.failure_count += 1
000299 |             self.last_failure_time = time.time()
000300 |             if self.failure_count >= self.failure_threshold and self.state != "OPEN":
000301 |                 self.state = "OPEN"
000302 |                 tripped = True
000303 | 
000304 |         if tripped and self.on_trip:
000305 |             self.on_trip()
000306 | 
000307 |     def allow_request(self) -> bool:
000308 |         with self._lock:
000309 |             if self.state == "OPEN":
000310 |                 if time.time() - self.last_failure_time > self.reset_timeout:
000311 |                     self.state = "HALF-OPEN"
000312 |                     return True
000313 |                 return False
000314 |             return True
000315 | 
000316 | 
000317 | class RetryPolicy:
000318 |     def __init__(
000319 |         self,
000320 |         max_attempts: int = 3,
000321 |         initial_backoff: float = 1.0,
000322 |         backoff_multiplier: float = 2.0,
000323 |         jitter_max: float = 0.3,
000324 |         sleeper: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
000325 |         rng: Callable[[float, float], float] = random.uniform,
000326 |     ) -> None:
000327 |         self.max_attempts = max_attempts
000328 |         self.initial_backoff = initial_backoff
000329 |         self.backoff_multiplier = backoff_multiplier
000330 |         self.jitter_max = jitter_max
000331 |         self.sleeper = sleeper
000332 |         self.rng = rng
000333 | 
000334 |     def execute(self, action: Callable[[], T]) -> T:
000335 |         delay = self.initial_backoff
000336 |         last_exception: Exception | None = None
000337 | 
000338 |         for attempt in range(1, self.max_attempts + 1):
000339 |             try:
000340 |                 return action()
000341 |             except Exception as exc:
000342 |                 last_exception = exc
000343 |                 if attempt == self.max_attempts:
000344 |                     break
000345 |                 jitter = self.rng(0.0, self.jitter_max) if self.jitter_max > 0.0 else 0.0
000346 |                 self.sleeper(delay + jitter)
000347 |                 delay *= self.backoff_multiplier
000348 | 
000349 |         raise last_exception or RuntimeError("Retry sequence terminated without exception state")
000350 |

000385 |     def generate_study_notes(self, raw_text: str, title: str) -> SummaryResult:
000386 |         start_time = time.perf_counter()
000387 |         bounded_text = str(raw_text or "").strip()[:MAX_INPUT_CHARS]
000388 |         bounded_title = str(title or "Study Notes").strip()[:MAX_TITLE_CHARS]
000389 | 
000390 |         if not self.circuit_breaker.allow_request():
000391 |             self.metrics.record_fallback()
000392 |             logger.warning("Circuit breaker OPEN; utilizing safe extractive synthesis")
000393 |             return self.fallback_generator.extractive_notes(bounded_text, bounded_title)
000394 | 
000395 |         prompt = (
000396 |             f"Generate rigorous academic study notes using this exact Markdown hierarchy:\n"
000397 |             f"# {bounded_title}\n## Executive Summary\n## Learning Objectives\n## Core Concepts\n"
000398 |             f"## Structured Study Notes\n### Core Mechanisms\n## Key Takeaways\n## Sources\n\n"
000399 |             f"SOURCE:\n{bounded_text[:16000]}"
000400 |         )
000401 | 
000402 |         def _perform_generation() -> SummaryResult:
000403 |             gen = self.summary_llm.generate(prompt=prompt, temperature=0.12, max_tokens=1800)
000404 |             markdown = f"<!-- {SCHEMA_VERSION} -->\n" + gen.response_text.strip()

000406 |                 markdown_notes=markdown,
000407 |                 model_name=gen.model_name,
000408 |                 tokens_used=gen.tokens_used,
000409 |             )
000410 |             return SummarySemanticValidator.validate(result)
000411 | 
000412 |         try:
000413 |             summary = self.retry_policy.execute(_perform_generation)
000414 |             self.circuit_breaker.record_success()

000416 |             self.metrics.record_summary(True, elapsed, summary.tokens_used or 0)
000417 |             return summary
000418 |         except Exception as exc:
000419 |             self.circuit_breaker.record_failure()

000421 |             self.metrics.record_summary(False, elapsed)
000422 |             self.metrics.record_fallback()

000424 |                 "Primary summary generation failed; fallback invoked",
000425 |                 extra={"context": {"error": str(exc)}},
000426 |             )

000453 |     def generate_study_assets(
000454 |         self,
000455 |         raw_text: str,
000456 |         title: str,
000457 |         flashcard_count: int = 10,
000458 |         mcq_count: int = 10,
000459 |     ) -> StudyAssetsResult:
000460 |         start_time = time.perf_counter()

000462 |         bounded_title = str(title or "Study Assets").strip()[:MAX_TITLE_CHARS]

000411 |     @staticmethod
000412 |     def _hash_text(text: str) -> str:
000413 |         return hashlib.sha256(text.strip().encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
000414 | 
000415 |     def embed(self, texts: Sequence[str]) -> np.ndarray:
000416 |         context = self._context(operation="embed", chunk_count=len(texts))
000417 | 
000418 |         if not texts:
000419 |             return np.empty((0, self.embedding_dim), dtype="float32")
000420 | 
000421 |         hashes = [self._hash_text(t) for t in texts]
000422 |         vectors: list[np.ndarray | None] = [None] * len(texts)
000423 |         missing_indices: list[int] = []
000424 |         missing_texts: list[str] = []
000425 |         now = time.time()
000426 | 
000427 |         try:
000428 |             placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in hashes)
000429 |             with self._get_connection() as conn:
000430 |                 cur = conn.execute(
000431 |                     f"""
000432 |                     SELECT text_hash, vector_blob
000433 |                     FROM embedding_cache
000434 |                     WHERE dimension = ? AND text_hash IN ({placeholders})
000435 |                     """,
000436 |                     [self.embedding_dim] + hashes,
000437 |                 )
000438 |                 cache_hits = {
000439 |                     row["text_hash"]: np.frombuffer(row["vector_blob"], dtype="float32")
000440 |                     for row in cur.fetchall()
000441 |                 }
000442 | 
000443 |                 for idx, h in enumerate(hashes):
000444 |                     if h in cache_hits:
000445 |                         vectors[idx] = cache_hits[h]
000446 |                     else:
000447 |                         missing_indices.append(idx)
000448 |                         missing_texts.append(texts[idx])
000449 | 
000450 |                 if cache_hits:
000451 |                     hit_placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in cache_hits)
000452 |                     conn.execute(
000453 |                         f"UPDATE embedding_cache SET last_accessed = ? WHERE text_hash IN ({hit_placeholders})",
000454 |                         [now] + list(cache_hits.keys()),
000455 |                     )
000456 | 
000457 |             if missing_texts:
000458 |                 normalize = self.similarity_metric == "cosine"
000459 |                 encoded = self.model.encode(
000460 |                     missing_texts,
000461 |                     convert_to_numpy=True,
000462 |                     normalize_embeddings=normalize,
000463 |                     show_progress_bar=False,
000464 |                 )
000465 |                 encoded = np.asarray(encoded, dtype="float32")
000466 | 
000467 |                 if encoded.ndim != 2 or encoded.shape[1] != self.embedding_dim:
000468 |                     raise RAGError(
000469 |                         "Embedding dimension mismatch during generation.",
000470 |                         context={
000471 |                             **context,
000472 |                             "error_type": "embedding_dimension_mismatch",
000473 |                             "actual_shape": tuple(encoded.shape),
000474 |                             "expected_dim": self.embedding_dim,
000475 |                         },
000476 |                     )
000477 | 
000478 |                 with self._get_connection() as conn:
000479 |                     for idx, vector in zip(missing_indices, encoded):
000480 |                         vectors[idx] = vector
000481 |                         conn.execute(
000482 |                             """
000483 |                             INSERT OR REPLACE INTO embedding_cache
000484 |                             (text_hash, vector_blob, dimension, last_accessed)
000485 |                             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
000486 |                             """,
000487 |                             (hashes[idx], vector.tobytes(), self.embedding_dim, now),
000488 |                         )
000489 | 
000490 |             return np.vstack(vectors).astype("float32")
000491 | 
000492 |         except RAGError:
000493 |             raise
000494 |         except Exception as exc:
000495 |             logger.exception("Embedding generation failed", extra={"context": context})
000496 |             raise RAGError("Embedding generation failed.", context=context) from exc
000497 | 
000498 |     def _row_or_dto_to_chunk_record(self, item: Any) -> _ChunkRecord:
000499 |         if isinstance(item, dict):
000500 |             data = item
000501 |         elif hasattr(item, "keys"):
000502 |             data = dict(item)
000503 |         elif is_dataclass(item):
000504 |             data = asdict(item)
000505 |         else:
000506 |             data = {
000507 |                 "chunk_id": getattr(item, "chunk_id", None),
000508 |                 "id": getattr(item, "id", None),
000509 |                 "document_id": getattr(item, "document_id", None),
000510 |                 "doc_id": getattr(item, "doc_id", None),
000511 |                 "text": getattr(item, "text", None),
000512 |                 "source_ref": getattr(item, "source_ref", None),
000513 |             }
000514 | 
000515 |         chunk_id = data.get("chunk_id", data.get("id"))
000516 |         document_id = data.get("document_id", data.get("doc_id"))
000517 | 
000518 |         if document_id is None:
000519 |             raise RAGError(
000520 |                 "Chunk record is missing document_id.",
000521 |                 context={"operation": "row_to_record", "error_type": "missing_document_id"},
000522 |             )
000523 | 
000524 |         text = data.get("text")
000525 |         if not isinstance(text, str) or not text.strip():
000526 |             raise RAGError(
000527 |                 "Chunk record is missing text.",
000528 |                 context={"operation": "row_to_record", "document_id": document_id, "error_type": "missing_text"},
000529 |             )
000530 | 
000531 |         return _ChunkRecord(
000532 |             chunk_id=int(chunk_id) if chunk_id is not None else None,
000533 |             document_id=int(document_id),
000534 |             text=text.strip(),
000535 |             source_ref=data.get("source_ref"),
000536 |         )
000537 | 
000538 |     def _fetch_chunks_for_document(self, document_id: int) -> list[_ChunkRecord]:
000539 |         context = self._context(operation="fetch_chunks_for_document", document_id=document_id)
000540 |         try:
000541 |             if hasattr(self.db, "get_chunks_typed"):
000542 |                 raw_chunks = self.db.get_chunks_typed(document_id)
000543 |             else:
000544 |                 raw_chunks = self.db.get_chunks(document_id)
000545 | 
000546 |             return [self._row_or_dto_to_chunk_record(chunk) for chunk in raw_chunks]
000547 |         except RAGError:
000548 |             raise
000549 |         except Exception as exc:
000550 |             logger.exception("Failed to fetch document chunks from database", extra={"context": context})
000551 |             raise RAGError("Failed to fetch document chunks from database.", context=context) from exc
000552 | 
000553 |     def _list_document_ids(self) -> list[int]:
000554 |         context = self._context(operation="list_document_ids")
000555 |         try:
000556 |             if hasattr(self.db, "list_documents_typed"):
000557 |                 docs = self.db.list_documents_typed(limit=100_000, offset=0)
000558 |             else:
000559 |                 docs = self.db.list_documents()
000560 | 
000561 |             doc_ids: list[int] = []
000562 |             for doc in docs:
000563 |                 val = doc.get("id") if isinstance(doc, dict) or hasattr(doc, "keys") else getattr(doc, "id", None)
000564 |                 if val is not None:
000565 |                     doc_ids.append(int(val))
000566 |             return doc_ids
000567 |         except Exception as exc:
000568 |             logger.exception("Failed to list document IDs from database", extra={"context": context})
000569 |             raise RAGError("Failed to list document IDs from database.", context=context) from exc
000570 | 
000571 |     def has_document(self, document_id: int) -> bool:
000572 |         with self._get_connection() as conn:
000573 |             cur = conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM rag_chunks WHERE document_id = ? LIMIT 1", (document_id,))
000574 |             return cur.fetchone() is not None
000575 | 
000576 |     def index_document(self, document_id: int, *, permitted_chunk_ids: Collection[int] | None = None) -> None:
000577 |         context = self._context(operation="index_document", document_id=document_id)
000578 | 
000579 |         with self._rw_lock:
000580 |             try:
000581 |                 chunks = self._fetch_chunks_for_document(document_id)
000582 |                 if permitted_chunk_ids is not None:
000583 |                     allow = frozenset(int(value) for value in permitted_chunk_ids)
000584 |                     chunks = [chunk for chunk in chunks if chunk.chunk_id is not None and int(chunk.chunk_id) in allow]
000585 |                 if not chunks:
000586 |                     logger.info("No chunks found for document indexing", extra={"context": context})
000587 |                     return
000588 | 
000589 |                 self.remove_document(document_id)
000590 | 
000591 |                 texts_to_embed = [c.text for c in chunks]
000592 |                 vectors = self.embed(texts_to_embed)
000593 |                 now = time.time()
000594 | 
000595 |                 with self._get_connection() as conn:
000596 |                     cur = conn.cursor()
000597 |                     inserted_ids: list[int] = []
000598 |                     for c in chunks:
000599 |                         cur.execute(
000600 |                             """
000601 |                             INSERT INTO rag_chunks (chunk_id, document_id, text, source_ref, text_hash, created_at)
000602 |                             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
000603 |                             """,
000604 |                             (c.chunk_id, c.document_id, c.text, c.source_ref, self._hash_text(c.text), now),
000605 |                         )
000606 |                         inserted_ids.append(int(cur.lastrowid))
000607 | 
000608 |                 index = self._require_index(operation="index_document")
000609 |                 ids_array = np.array(inserted_ids, dtype=np.int64)
000610 |                 index.add_with_ids(vectors, ids_array)
000611 |                 self.save()
000612 | 
000613 |                 logger.info(
000614 |                     "Document indexed incrementally",
000615 |                     extra={"context": self._context(operation="index_document_success", chunk_count=len(chunks))},
000616 |                 )
000617 |             except RAGError:
000618 |                 raise
000619 |             except Exception as exc:
000620 |                 logger.exception("Document indexing failed", extra={"context": context})
000621 |                 raise RAGError("Document indexing failed.", context=context) from exc
000622 | 
000623 |     def remove_document(self, document_id: int) -> None:
000624 |         context = self._context(operation="remove_document", document_id=document_id)
000625 | 
000626 |         with self._rw_lock:
000627 |             try:
000628 |                 with self._get_connection() as conn:
000629 |                     cur = conn.execute("SELECT faiss_id FROM rag_chunks WHERE document_id = ?", (document_id,))
000630 |                     rows = cur.fetchall()
000631 |                     if not rows:
000632 |                         return
000633 |                     ids_to_remove = [int(r["faiss_id"]) for r in rows]
000634 |                     conn.execute("DELETE FROM rag_chunks WHERE document_id = ?", (document_id,))
000635 | 
000636 |                 if self.index is not None and ids_to_remove:
000637 |                     ids_array = np.array(ids_to_remove, dtype=np.int64)
000638 |                     self.index.remove_ids(ids_array)
000639 |                     self.save()
000640 | 
000641 |                 logger.info(
000642 |                     "Document removed from index and metadata store",
000643 |                     extra={"context": self._context(operation="remove_document_success", removed_count=len(ids_to_remove))},
000644 |                 )
000645 |             except RAGError:
000646 |                 raise
000647 |             except Exception as exc:
000648 |                 logger.exception("Document removal failed", extra={"context": context})
000649 |                 raise RAGError("Document removal failed.", context=context) from exc
000650 | 
000651 |     def rebuild_from_source_documents(self) -> None:
000652 |         context = self._context(operation="rebuild_from_source_documents")
000653 | 
000654 |         with self._rw_lock:
000655 |             try:
000656 |                 with self._get_connection() as conn:
000657 |                     conn.execute("DELETE FROM rag_chunks;")
000658 | 
000659 |                 self.index = self._create_base_index()
000660 | 
000661 |                 doc_ids = self._list_document_ids()
000662 |                 for did in doc_ids:
000663 |                     chunks = self._fetch_chunks_for_document(did)
000664 |                     if not chunks:
000665 |                         continue
000666 | 
000667 |                     texts_to_embed = [c.text for c in chunks]
000668 |                     vectors = self.embed(texts_to_embed)
000669 |                     now = time.time()
000670 | 
000671 |                     with self._get_connection() as conn:
000672 |                         cur = conn.cursor()
000673 |                         inserted_ids: list[int] = []
000674 |                         for c in chunks:
000675 |                             cur.execute(
000676 |                                 """
000677 |                                 INSERT INTO rag_chunks (chunk_id, document_id, text, source_ref, text_hash, created_at)
000678 |                                 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
000679 |                                 """,
000680 |                                 (c.chunk_id, c.document_id, c.text, c.source_ref, self._hash_text(c.text), now),
000681 |                             )
000682 |                             inserted_ids.append(int(cur.lastrowid))
000683 | 
000684 |                     ids_array = np.array(inserted_ids, dtype=np.int64)
000685 |                     self.index.add_with_ids(vectors, ids_array)
000686 | 
000687 |                 self.save()
000688 |                 logger.info(
000689 |                     "RAG engine fully rebuilt from source documents",
000690 |                     extra={"context": self._context(operation="rebuild_success", document_count=len(doc_ids))},
000691 |                 )
000692 |             except RAGError:
000693 |                 raise
000694 |             except Exception as exc:
000695 |                 logger.exception("Rebuilding RAG engine from sources failed", extra={"context": context})
000696 |                 raise RAGError("Rebuilding RAG engine from sources failed.", context=context) from exc
000697 | 
000698 |     @staticmethod
000699 |     def normalize_query(query: str) -> str:
000700 |         cleaned = query.strip().lower()
000701 |         cleaned = re.sub(r"\bconfidential\b", "", cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
000702 |         return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", cleaned).strip()
000703 | 
000704 |     def _normalize_score(self, raw_score: float) -> float:
000705 |         if self.similarity_metric == "cosine":
000706 |             return max(0.0, min(1.0, (float(raw_score) + 1.0) / 2.0))
000707 |         return 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-float(raw_score)))
000708 | 
000709 |     def search(
000710 |         self,
000711 |         query: str,
000712 |         top_k: int = 5,
000713 |         min_similarity: float = DEFAULT_MIN_SIMILARITY,
000714 |     ) -> list[RetrievedChunk]:
000715 |         normalized_query = self.normalize_query(query)
000716 |         context = self._context(
000717 |             operation="search",
000718 |             query=normalized_query,
000719 |             top_k=top_k,
000720 |             min_similarity=min_similarity,
000721 |         )
000722 | 
000723 |         if not normalized_query:
000724 |             raise RAGError("Search query must be non-empty.", context={**context, "error_type": "validation_error"})
000725 | 
000726 |         if top_k <= 0:
000727 |             raise RAGError("top_k must be greater than zero.", context={**context, "error_type": "validation_error"})
000728 | 
000729 |         try:
000730 |             q_vec = self.embed([normalized_query])
000731 | 
000732 |             with self._rw_lock:
000733 |                 index = self._require_index(operation="search")
000734 |                 if index.ntotal == 0:
000735 |                     return []
000736 | 
000737 |                 scores, idxs = index.search(q_vec, min(top_k, index.ntotal))
000738 | 
000739 |             valid_hits: list[tuple[int, float]] = []
000740 |             for raw_score, fid in zip(scores[0], idxs[0]):
000741 |                 if fid == -1:
000742 |                     continue
000743 |                 score = self._normalize_score(raw_score)
000744 |                 if score >= min_similarity:
000745 |                     valid_hits.append((int(fid), round(score, 4)))
000746 | 
000747 |             if not valid_hits:
000748 |                 return []
000749 | 
000750 |             id_order = {fid: (idx, score) for idx, (fid, score) in enumerate(valid_hits)}
000751 |             placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in valid_hits)
000752 |             fid_params = [fid for fid, _ in valid_hits]
000753 | 
000754 |             with self._get_connection() as conn:
000755 |                 cur = conn.execute(
000756 |                     f"""
000757 |                     SELECT faiss_id, chunk_id, document_id, text, source_ref
000758 |                     FROM rag_chunks
000759 |                     WHERE faiss_id IN ({placeholders})
000760 |                     """,
000761 |                     fid_params,
000762 |                 )
000763 |                 rows = cur.fetchall()
000764 | 
000765 |             matched_chunks: list[tuple[int, RetrievedChunk]] = []
000766 |             for row in rows:
000767 |                 order_idx, score = id_order[row["faiss_id"]]
000768 |                 chunk_dict = {
000769 |                     "chunk_id": row["chunk_id"],
000770 |                     "document_id": int(row["document_id"]),
000771 |                     "doc_id": int(row["document_id"]),
000772 |                     "text": row["text"],
000773 |                     "source_ref": row["source_ref"],
000774 |                     "score": score,
000775 |                 }
000776 |                 matched_chunks.append(
000777 |                     (
000778 |                         order_idx,
000779 |                         self._build_dto(RetrievedChunk, chunk_dict),
000780 |                     )
000781 |                 )
000782 | 
000783 |             matched_chunks.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
000784 |             results = [chunk for _, chunk in matched_chunks]
000785 | 
000786 |             logger.info(
000787 |                 "RAG search completed",
000788 |                 extra={"context": {**context, "hits_returned": len(results)}},
000789 |             )
000790 |             return results
000791 | 
000792 |         except RAGError:
000793 |             raise
000794 |         except Exception as exc:
000795 |             logger.exception("RAG search failed", extra={"context": context})
000796 |             raise RAGError("RAG search failed.", context=context) from exc
000797 | 
000798 |     @staticmethod
000799 |     def _summarize_context_for_prompt(
000800 |         hits: Sequence[RetrievedChunk],
000801 |         *,
000802 |         max_chars_per_hit: int = 1_500,
000803 |     ) -> str:
000804 |         sections: list[str] = []
000805 |         for hit in hits:
000806 |             source = hit.source_ref or f"document:{getattr(hit, 'document_id', getattr(hit, 'doc_id', 'unknown'))}"
000807 |             text = hit.text.strip()
000808 |             if len(text) > max_chars_per_hit:
000809 |                 text = text[:max_chars_per_hit].rsplit(" ", 1)[0].strip() + "..."
000810 |             sections.append(f"[Source: {source} | score={hit.score:.3f}]\n{text}")
000811 |         return "\n\n".join(sections).strip()
000812 | 
000813 |     @staticmethod
000814 |     def _unique_sources(hits: Sequence[RetrievedChunk]) -> list[str]:
000815 |         return list(dict.fromkeys(hit.source_ref for hit in hits if hit.source_ref))
000816 | 
000817 |     def answer(
000818 |         self,
000819 |         question: str,
000820 |         llm: OllamaClient | None = None,
000821 |         *,
000822 |         top_k: int = 5,
000823 |         min_similarity: float = DEFAULT_MIN_SIMILARITY,
000824 |         max_tokens: int = 1200,
000825 |     ) -> AnswerResult:
000826 |         cleaned_question = question.strip()
000827 |         context = self._context(
000828 |             operation="answer",
000829 |             question=cleaned_question,
000830 |             top_k=top_k,
000831 |             min_similarity=min_similarity,
000832 |         )
000833 | 
000834 |         if not cleaned_question:
000835 |             raise RAGError("Question must be non-empty.", context={**context, "error_type": "validation_error"})
000836 | 
000837 |         client = llm or OllamaClient()
000838 | 
000839 |         try:
000840 |             client.healthcheck()
000841 | 
000842 |             hits = self.search(cleaned_question, top_k=top_k, min_similarity=min_similarity)
000843 |             retrieved_context = self._summarize_context_for_prompt(hits)
000844 |             sources = self._unique_sources(hits)
000845 |             avg_score = round(sum(h.score for h in hits) / len(hits), 4) if hits else 0.0
000846 | 
000847 |             prompt = f"""
000848 | Answer the student question using only the retrieved context.
000849 | 
000850 | If the answer is not in the context, say exactly:
000851 | "I could not find enough information in the uploaded study materials."
000852 | 
000853 | Rules:
000854 | - Be accurate and concise.
000855 | - Cite source anchors inline when possible.
000856 | - Do not invent facts.
000857 | - If multiple sources support the answer, mention them.
000858 | 
000859 | Question:
000860 | {cleaned_question}
000861 | 
000862 | Retrieved Context:
000863 | {retrieved_context if retrieved_context else "No relevant context retrieved."}
000864 | """.strip()
000865 | 
000866 |             logger.info(
000867 |                 "RAG answer generation started",
000868 |                 extra={
000869 |                     "context": {
000870 |                         **context,
000871 |                         "context_length": len(retrieved_context),
000872 |                         "hits_count": len(hits),
000873 |                         "avg_score": avg_score,
000874 |                         "model": client.model,
000875 |                         "tokens_requested": max_tokens,
000876 |                     }
000877 |                 },
000878 |             )
000879 | 
000880 |             generation = client.generate(
000881 |                 prompt=prompt,
000882 |                 system="You are AmbaAI Tutor. Be accurate, helpful, and cite source anchors.",
000883 |                 temperature=0.1,
000884 |                 max_tokens=max_tokens,
000885 |             )
000886 | 
000887 |             result = self._build_dto(
000888 |                 AnswerResult,
000889 |                 {
000890 |                     "answer_text": generation.response_text,
000891 |                     "sources": sources,
000892 |                     "model_name": generation.model_name,
000893 |                     "metadata": {
000894 |                         "query_length": len(cleaned_question),
000895 |                         "hits_count": len(hits),
000896 |                         "avg_score": avg_score,
000897 |                         "top_k": top_k,
000898 |                         "min_similarity": min_similarity,
000899 |                         "context_length": len(retrieved_context),
000900 |                         "tokens_used": generation.tokens_used,
000901 |                         "llm_metadata": generation.metadata,
000902 |                     },
000903 |                 },
000904 |             )
000905 | 
000906 |             logger.info(
000907 |                 "RAG answer generation completed",
000908 |                 extra={
000909 |                     "context": {
000910 |                         **context,
000911 |                         "answer_length": len(generation.response_text),
000912 |                         "sources_count": len(sources),
000913 |                         "hits_count": len(hits),
000914 |                         "avg_score": avg_score,
000915 |                         "model_name": generation.model_name,
000916 |                         "tokens_used": generation.tokens_used,
000917 |                     }
000918 |                 },
000919 |             )
000920 | 
000921 |             return result
000922 | 
000923 |         except ModelUnavailableError:
000924 |             raise
000925 |         except RAGError:
000926 |             raise
000927 |         except Exception as exc:
000928 |             logger.exception("RAG answer generation failed", extra={"context": context})
000929 |             raise RAGError("RAG answer generation failed.", context=context) from exc

000011 | def _coerce_bool(value: Any, default: bool) -> bool:
000012 |     if value is None:
000013 |         return default
000014 |     if isinstance(value, bool):
000015 |         return value
000016 |     if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
000017 |         return bool(value)
000018 |     return str(value).strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}

000021 | class SummaryMode(str, Enum):
000022 |     SHORT = "Short Summary"
000023 |     DETAILED = "Detailed Summary"
000024 |     KEY_CONCEPTS = "Key Concepts"
000025 |     TEACHER_REVIEW = "Teacher Review Draft"
000026 |     LEARNER_FRIENDLY = "Learner-Friendly Summary"
000027 | 
000028 |     @classmethod
000029 |     def values(cls) -> list[str]:
000030 |         return [item.value for item in cls]
000031 | 
000032 |     @classmethod
000033 |     def coerce(cls, value: str | None) -> SummaryMode:
000034 |         if value is None:
000035 |             return cls.DETAILED
000036 |         for item in cls:
000037 |             if str(value).strip().lower() == item.value.lower():
000038 |                 return item
000039 |         return cls.DETAILED

000092 |     @classmethod
000093 |     def from_raw(cls, markdown: str, metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> ParsedLessonBundle:
000094 |         meta = dict(metadata or {})
000095 |         raw = str(markdown or "").strip()
000096 | 
000097 |         source_badge = ""
000098 |         page_match = re.search(r"\[Page\s*(\d+)\]", raw, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
000099 |         if page_match:
000100 |             source_badge = f"Page {page_match.group(1)}"
000101 |             raw = re.sub(r"\[Page\s*\d+\]\s*", "", raw, flags=re.IGNORECASE).strip()
000102 | 
000103 |         title = str(meta.get("title") or "")
000104 |         if not title:
000105 |             first_line = raw.split("\n")[0].strip()
000106 |             if first_line.startswith("#"):
000107 |                 title = first_line.lstrip("#").strip()
000108 |             else:
000109 |                 title_match = re.search(r"^(.*?)(?:The\s+jq\s+utility|is\s+not\s+just|\.|\n|$)", raw, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
000110 |                 if title_match:
000111 |                     title = title_match.group(1).strip()
000112 |         if not title:
000113 |             title = "Command-Line Safety & Parsing"
000114 | 
000115 |         cleaned_text = re.sub(r"^#+\s*.*?\n", "", raw).strip()
000116 | 
000117 |         bottom_line = ""
000118 |         bl_match = re.search(r"(?:💎|💡|Bottom line:?)\s*(.*?)(?=\n|$)", cleaned_text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
000119 |         if bl_match:
000120 |             bottom_line = bl_match.group(1).strip()
000121 |             cleaned_text = cleaned_text[:bl_match.start()] + cleaned_text[bl_match.end():]
000122 | 
000123 |         intro_match = re.search(r"^(.*?)(?=(?:🎯|🔍|🛡|⚡|📌|\b\d+\.|\n##|\n###))", cleaned_text, flags=re.DOTALL)
000124 |         if intro_match:
000125 |             exec_summary = intro_match.group(1).strip()
000126 |             rest = cleaned_text[intro_match.end():].strip()
000127 |         else:
000128 |             exec_summary = cleaned_text[:200].strip()
000129 |             rest = cleaned_text[200:].strip()
000130 | 
000131 |         sections: list[LessonSectionModel] = []
000132 | 
000133 |         step_pattern = re.compile(
000134 |             r"(?:🎯|🔍|🛡|⚡|📌|\b)\s*(\d+)\.\s*([^:\n]+)[:\.\-]?\s*(.*?)(?=(?:🎯|🔍|🛡|⚡|📌|\b)\s*\d+\.|\Z)",
000135 |             flags=re.DOTALL,
000136 |         )
000137 |         matches = list(step_pattern.finditer(rest))
000138 | 
000139 |         if matches:
000140 |             for m in matches:
000141 |                 sec_title = m.group(2).strip()
000142 |                 content = m.group(3).strip()
000143 | 
000144 |                 code_snippets: list[dict[str, str]] = []
000145 |                 codes = re.findall(r"(curl\s+-[^\n]+|cat\s+[^\n]+|diff\s+<[^\n]+|jq\s+[^\n]+)", content)
000146 |                 for c in codes:
000147 |                     code_clean = c.strip().rstrip("'\"")
000148 |                     code_snippets.append({
000149 |                         "language": "bash",
000150 |                         "code": code_clean,
000151 |                         "title": "Terminal Execution",
000152 |                     })
000153 | 
000154 |                 clean_content = content
000155 |                 for c in codes:
000156 |                     clean_content = clean_content.replace(c, "").strip()
000157 |                 clean_content = re.sub(r"['\"]{2,}", "", clean_content).strip()
000158 | 
000159 |                 tags = []
000160 |                 if "api" in sec_title.lower():
000161 |                     tags.extend(["API", "JSON"])
000162 |                 if "secret" in sec_title.lower() or "token" in sec_title.lower():
000163 |                     tags.extend(["Security", "Regex"])
000164 |                 if "config" in sec_title.lower() or "diff" in sec_title.lower():
000165 |                     tags.extend(["Diff", "DevOps"])
000166 | 
000167 |                 sections.append(
000168 |                     LessonSectionModel(
000169 |                         title=sec_title,
000170 |                         summary_text=clean_content,
000171 |                         code_blocks=code_snippets,
000172 |                         tags=tags,
000173 |                         bottom_line=bottom_line if not sections else "",
000174 |                     )
000175 |                 )
000176 |         else:
000177 |             code_snippets: list[dict[str, str]] = []
000178 |             codes = re.findall(r"(curl\s+-[^\n]+|cat\s+[^\n]+|diff\s+<[^\n]+|jq\s+[^\n]+)", rest)
000179 |             for c in codes:
000180 |                 code_snippets.append({
000181 |                     "language": "bash",
000182 |                     "code": c.strip(),
000183 |                     "title": "Terminal Command",
000184 |                 })

000187 |                 LessonSectionModel(

000190 |                     code_blocks=code_snippets,

000001 | from __future__ import annotations
000002 | 
000003 | from typing import Any
000004 | 
000005 | from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, QThreadPool, Signal, Slot
000006 | 
000007 | from amba_ai.logger import logger
000008 | from amba_ai.models.schemas import SummaryResult
000009 | from amba_ai.services.job_service import JobService
000010 | from .data_classes import SummarizerRequest, SummaryViewModel
000011 | from amba_ai.ui.workers import FunctionWorker, unwrap_worker_result
000012 | 
000013 | 
000014 | class SummarizerManager(QObject):
000015 |     summaryStarted = Signal(str)
000016 |     summaryFinished = Signal(dict)
000017 |     summaryFailed = Signal(dict)
000018 |     summaryGenerated = Signal(int)
000019 |     statusMessage = Signal(str, str)
000020 |     operationStarted = Signal(str)
000021 |     operationFinished = Signal(str)
000022 | 
000023 |     def __init__(self, job_service: JobService, parent: QObject | None = None) -> None:
000024 |         super().__init__(parent)
000025 |         self.jobs = job_service
000026 |         self.thread_pool = QThreadPool.globalInstance()
000027 |         self._is_operation_active = False
000028 |         self._equity_profile: dict[str, Any] = {}
000029 | 
000030 |     @Slot(dict)
000031 |     def apply_equity_profile(self, profile: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
000032 |         self._equity_profile = dict(profile or {})
000033 |         self.statusMessage.emit("Summarizer profile synchronized with Equity Engine.", "INFO")
000034 | 
000035 |     @Slot(dict)
000036 |     def generate_summary(self, request_payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
000037 |         if self._is_operation_active:
000038 |             self.statusMessage.emit("Curriculum synthesis already in progress.", "WARNING")
000039 |             return
000040 | 
000041 |         request = SummarizerRequest.from_dict(request_payload)
000042 | 
000043 |         if request.document_id <= 0:
000044 |             context = {
000045 |                 "task_name": "Synthesize Lesson",
000046 |                 "error_type": "InvalidDocument",
000047 |                 "error_message": "A valid document must be selected before lesson generation.",
000048 |             }
000049 |             self.summaryFailed.emit(context)
000050 |             self.statusMessage.emit(context["error_message"], "ERROR")
000051 |             return
000052 | 
000053 |         self._is_operation_active = True
000054 |         self.summaryStarted.emit(request.mode.value)
000055 |         self.operationStarted.emit("Synthesizing structured lesson modules...")
000056 | 
000057 |         worker = FunctionWorker(
000058 |             self._run_summary,
000059 |             request,
000060 |             task_name="Synthesize Lesson",
000061 |             max_retries=0,
000062 |         )
000063 |         worker.signals.finished.connect(self._on_summary_finished)
000064 |         worker.signals.error.connect(self._on_worker_error)
000065 |         worker.signals.status.connect(self._on_worker_status)
000066 | 
000067 |         self.thread_pool.start(worker)
000068 | 
000069 |     def _run_summary(self, request: SummarizerRequest) -> SummaryViewModel:
000070 |         if hasattr(self.jobs, "summarize_document"):
000071 |             result = self.jobs.summarize_document(
000072 |                 request.document_id,
000073 |                 force=request.force,
000074 |             )
000075 |         elif hasattr(self.jobs, "generate_notes"):
000076 |             result = self.jobs.generate_notes(
000077 |                 request.document_id,
000078 |                 force=request.force,
000079 |                 model_name=request.preferred_model,
000080 |             )
000081 |         else:
000082 |             raise AttributeError("JobService does not support document summarization.")
000083 | 
000084 |         if not isinstance(result, SummaryResult):
000085 |             raise TypeError("Summarizer backend returned an invalid result.")
000086 | 
000087 |         return SummaryViewModel.from_summary_result(result, mode=request.mode.value)
000088 | 
000089 |     @Slot(dict)
000090 |     def _on_worker_status(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
000091 |         message = str(payload.get("message") or payload.get("stage") or "Processing lesson context...")
000092 |         self.statusMessage.emit(message, "INFO")
000093 | 
000094 |     @Slot(dict)
000095 |     def _on_worker_error(self, error_context: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
000096 |         self._is_operation_active = False
000097 |         logger.warning("Summarizer worker emitted error", extra={"context": error_context})
000098 |         message = str(error_context.get("error_message") or "Lesson generation failed.")
000099 |         self.summaryFailed.emit(error_context)
000100 |         self.statusMessage.emit(message, "ERROR")
000101 |         self.operationFinished.emit("Lesson generation failed.")
000102 | 
000103 |     @Slot(object)

000132 |                 working_content = clean_s
000133 | 
000134 |         if not working_content:
000135 |             working_content = content
000136 | 
000137 |         key_takeaway = ""

000139 |         if bl_match:

000141 |             working_content = working_content[:bl_match.start()] + working_content[bl_match.end():]

000014 | class NotesManager(QObject):
000015 |     notesStarted = Signal(str)
000016 |     notesFinished = Signal(dict)
000017 |     notesFailed = Signal(dict)
000018 |     notesGenerated = Signal(int)
000019 |     statusMessage = Signal(str, str)
000020 |     operationStarted = Signal(str)
000021 |     operationFinished = Signal(str)
000022 | 
000023 |     def __init__(self, job_service: JobService, parent: QObject | None = None) -> None:
000024 |         super().__init__(parent)
000025 |         self.jobs = job_service
000026 |         self.thread_pool = QThreadPool.globalInstance()
000027 |         self._is_operation_active = False
000028 |         self._equity_profile: dict[str, Any] = {}
000029 | 
000030 |     @Slot(dict)
000031 |     def apply_equity_profile(self, profile: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
000032 |         self._equity_profile = dict(profile or {})
000033 |         self.statusMessage.emit("Notes cognitive profile synchronized with Equity Switch.", "INFO")
000034 | 
000035 |     @Slot(dict)
000036 |     def generate_notes(self, request_payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
000037 |         if self._is_operation_active:
000038 |             self.statusMessage.emit("Notes generation already in progress.", "WARNING")
000039 |             return
000040 | 
000041 |         request = NotesGenerationRequest.from_dict(request_payload)
000042 | 
000043 |         if request.document_id <= 0:
000044 |             context = {
000045 |                 "task_name": "Generate Notes",
000046 |                 "error_type": "InvalidDocument",
000047 |                 "error_message": "A valid document must be selected before generating study notes.",
000048 |             }
000049 |             self.notesFailed.emit(context)
000050 |             self.statusMessage.emit(context["error_message"], "ERROR")
000051 |             return
000052 | 
000053 |         self._is_operation_active = True
000054 |         self.notesStarted.emit(request.note_style)
000055 |         self.operationStarted.emit("Synthesizing active study notes & interactive curriculum...")
000056 | 
000057 |         worker = FunctionWorker(
000058 |             self._run_notes,
000059 |             request,
000060 |             task_name="Generate Notes",
000061 |             max_retries=0,
000062 |         )
000063 |         worker.signals.finished.connect(self._on_notes_finished)
000064 |         worker.signals.error.connect(self._on_worker_error)
000065 |         worker.signals.status.connect(self._on_worker_status)
000066 | 
000067 |         self.thread_pool.start(worker)
000068 | 
000069 |     def _run_notes(self, request: NotesGenerationRequest) -> NotesViewModel:
000070 |         try:
000071 |             result = self.jobs.generate_notes(
000072 |                 request.document_id,
000073 |                 force=request.force,
000074 |                 model_name=request.preferred_model,
000075 |             )
000076 |         except TypeError:
000077 |             result = self.jobs.generate_notes(
000078 |                 request.document_id,
000079 |                 force=request.force,
000080 |             )
000081 | 
000082 |         if not isinstance(result, SummaryResult):
000083 |             raise TypeError("Notes backend returned an invalid result.")
000084 | 
000085 |         return NotesViewModel.from_result(result, note_style=request.note_style)
000086 | 
000087 |     @Slot(dict)
000088 |     def _on_worker_status(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
000089 |         message = str(payload.get("message") or payload.get("stage") or "Processing note synthesis...")
000090 |         self.statusMessage.emit(message, "INFO")
000091 |

000011 | def _coerce_bool(value: Any, default: bool) -> bool:
000012 |     if value is None:
000013 |         return default
000014 |     if isinstance(value, bool):
000015 |         return value
000016 |     if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
000017 |         return bool(value)
000018 |     return str(value).strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}

000020 |

000022 |     SOCRATIC = "Socratic Tutor"

000029 |         return [item.value for item in cls]

000031 |     @classmethod
000032 |     def coerce(cls, value: str | None) -> TutorPersona:
000033 |         if value is None:
000034 |             return cls.SIMPLE
000035 |         for item in cls:
000036 |             if str(value).strip().lower() == item.value.lower():
000037 |                 return item

000283 |     @classmethod
000284 |     def _parse_and_enrich_answer(
000285 |         cls,
000286 |         raw_text: str,
000287 |         question: str,
000288 |         persona: TutorPersona,
000289 |     ) -> tuple[str, list[str], list[TutorCodeSnippet]]:
000290 |         text = str(raw_text or "").strip()
000291 |         text = re.sub(r"\[Page\s*\d+\]\s*", "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
000292 | 
000293 |         code_snippets: list[TutorCodeSnippet] = []
000294 |         codes = re.findall(
000295 |             r"(?:```(?:bash|sh|json)?\n(.*?)\n```|(curl\s+-[^\n]+|cat\s+[^\n]+|diff\s+<[^\n]+|jq\s+['\"][^\n]+['\"]|jq\s+[^\n]+))",
000296 |             text,
000297 |             flags=re.DOTALL,
000298 |         )
000299 | 
000300 |         for match_tuple in codes:
000301 |             code_match = match_tuple[0] or match_tuple[1]
000302 |             if code_match and code_match.strip():
000303 |                 clean_c = code_match.strip().rstrip("'\"")
000304 |                 lang = "bash" if any(k in clean_c for k in ("curl", "jq", "cat", "diff")) else "json"
000305 |                 code_snippets.append(
000306 |                     TutorCodeSnippet(
000307 |                         code=clean_c,
000308 |                         language=lang,
000309 |                         title="CLI Terminal Execution",
000310 |                     )
000311 |                 )
000312 | 
000313 |         clean_text = re.sub(r"```.*?```", "", text, flags=re.DOTALL).strip()
000314 |         for s in code_snippets:
000315 |             clean_text = clean_text.replace(s.code, "").strip()
000316 | 
000317 |         clean_text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", clean_text)
000318 |         if not clean_text:
000319 |             clean_text = "Here is the concise synthesis based on your grounded curriculum material."
000320 | 
000321 |         key_points: list[str] = []
000322 |         bullet_matches = re.findall(r"(?:^|\n)[-•*✦]\s*(.+?)(?=\n[-•*✦]|\n\n|\Z)", clean_text)
000323 |         if bullet_matches:
000324 |             key_points = [b.strip() for b in bullet_matches[:4] if len(b.strip()) > 5]
000325 | 
000326 |         return clean_text, key_points, code_snippets

000035 |     @Slot(dict)

000037 |         if self._is_operation_active:

000039 |             return
000040 |

000042 |

000047 |                 "error_message": "Please enter a valid study question or concept.",
000048 |             }
000049 |             self.tutorFailed.emit(context)
000050 |             self.statusMessage.emit(context["error_message"], "ERROR")
000051 |             return

000059 |             request,
000060 |             task_name="Tutor Explanation",
000061 |             max_retries=0,
000062 |         )

000064 |         worker.signals.error.connect(self._on_worker_error)

000066 |

000069 |     def _run_tutor_answer(self, request: TutorRequest) -> TutorAnswerViewModel:
000070 |         if hasattr(self.jobs, "tutor_chat"):

000074 |                 due_flashcards=request.due_flashcards, session_id=request.session_id,
000075 |             )
000076 |         elif hasattr(self.jobs, "tutor_answer"):
000077 |             result = self.jobs.tutor_answer(
000078 |                 request.question, top_k=request.top_k, max_tokens=request.max_tokens, document_id=request.document_id,

000015 | 
000016 | class Completion:
000017 |     def __init__(self, *, acknowledged: bool = True) -> None:
000018 |         self.acknowledged = acknowledged
000019 | 
000020 |     def require_delivery_acknowledged(self) -> None:
000021 |         if not self.acknowledged:
000022 |             raise RuntimeError("not acknowledged")
000023 | 
000024 | 
000025 | class Coordinator(JobGovernanceCoordinator):
000026 |     def __init__(self, *, block: bool = False, acknowledged: bool = True) -> None:
000027 |         self.block = block
000028 |         self.acknowledged = acknowledged
000029 |         self.events: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
000030 | 
000031 |     def begin(self, *, method_name: str, input_metadata: Any, context: Any) -> Any:
000032 |         self.events.append(("begin", method_name))
000033 |         if self.block:
000034 |             raise JobGovernanceGateError(
000035 |                 method_name=method_name,
000036 |                 operation_id="classroom_instruction_delivery",
000037 |                 event_id="event-1",
000038 |                 action=__import__(
000039 |                     "amba_ai.governance.models", fromlist=["EnforcementAction"]
000040 |                 ).EnforcementAction.BLOCK,
000041 |             )
000042 |         return object()
000043 | 
000044 |     def complete(self, *, invocation: Any, result_material: Any, result_metadata: Any) -> Completion:
000045 |         self.events.append(("complete", str(result_metadata["output_type"])))
000046 |         return Completion(acknowledged=self.acknowledged)
000047 |

000054 |         return SimpleNamespace()

000058 |         yield "first"
000059 |         yield " second"
